        self.INS_UMBRELLA: bool = False
        
        self.INV_STOCKS: bool = False
        self.INV_BONDS: bool = False
        self.INV_MUTUAL_FUND: bool = False
        self.INV_ETF: bool = False
        self.INV_OPTIONS: bool = False

    def main_control(self):
        """0000-MAIN-CONTROL."""
        self.initialization()  # PERFORM 1000-INITIALIZATION
        self.process_banking()  # PERFORM 2000-PROCESS-BANKING
        self.process_loans()  # PERFORM 3000-PROCESS-LOANS
        self.process_insurance()  # PERFORM 4000-PROCESS-INSURANCE
        self.process_investments()  # PERFORM 5000-PROCESS-INVESTMENTS
        self.generate_reports()  # PERFORM 6000-GENERATE-REPORTS
        self.termination()  # PERFORM 9000-TERMINATION
        print("STOP RUN.")

    def initialization(self):
        """1000-INITIALIZATION."""
        self.open_files()  # PERFORM 1100-OPEN-FILES
        self.initialize_counters()  # PERFORM 1200-INITIALIZE-COUNTERS
        self.get_current_date()  # PERFORM 1300-GET-CURRENT-DATE
        self.load_parameters()  # PERFORM 1400-LOAD-PARAMETERS

from dataclasses import dataclass

class MegaEnterpriseSystem:

           PERFORM 4100-PROCESS-POLICIES
           PERFORM 4200-CALCULATE-PREMIUMS
           PERFORM 4300-PROCESS-CLAIMS
           PERFORM 4400-ASSESS-RISK
           PERFORM 4500-RENEW-POLICIES.

        self.process_policies()
        self.calculate_premiums()
        self.process_claims()
        self.assess_risk()
        self.renew_policies()

    def process_policies(self) -> None:
        """4100-PROCESS-POLICIES.
           DISPLAY "PROCESSING INSURANCE POLICIES..."
           CONTINUE.
        """
        print("PROCESSING INSURANCE POLICIES...")

    def calculate_premiums(self) -> None:
        """4200-CALCULATE-PREMIUMS.
           DISPLAY "CALCULATING PREMIUMS..."
           SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
               READ INSURANCE-MASTER NEXT
                   AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
                   NOT AT END
                       PERFORM 4210-DETERMINE-BASE-PREMIUM
                       PERFORM 4220-APPLY-RISK-FACTOR
                       PERFORM 4230-CALCULATE-FINAL-PREMIUM
               END-READ
           END-PERFORM.
        """
        print("CALCULATING PREMIUMS...")
        self.WS_NOT_EOF = True
        while self.WS_NOT_EOF:
            try:
                self.INSURANCE_MASTER = self.INSURANCE_MASTER_FILE.pop(0)
                self.determine_base_premium()
                self.apply_risk_factor()
                self.calculate_final_premium()
            except IndexError:
                self.WS_EOF = True
                self.WS_NOT_EOF = False

    def determine_base_premium(self) -> None:
        """4210-DETERMINE-BASE-PREMIUM.
           EVALUATE TRUE
               WHEN INS-LIFE
                   COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = 
                       INS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT / 1000 * WS-LIFE-RATE-PER-1000
               WHEN INS-HEALTH
                   MOVE WS-HEALTH-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-CALC-AMOUNT
               WHEN INS-AUTO
                   MOVE WS-AUTO-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-CALC-AMOUNT
               WHEN INS-HOME
                   COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = 
                       INS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT / 1000 * WS-HOME-RATE-PER-1000
               WHEN INS-UMBRELLA
                   MOVE WS-UMBRELLA-RATE TO WS-CALC-AMOUNT
           END-EVALUATE.
        """
        match True:
            case self.INSURANCE_MASTER.INS_LIFE:
                self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = (self.INSURANCE_MASTER.INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT / 1000) * self.WS_LIFE_RATE_PER_1000
            case self.INSURANCE_MASTER.INS_HEALTH:
                self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = self.WS_HEALTH_BASE_PREMIUM
            case self.INSURANCE_MASTER.INS_AUTO:
                self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = self.WS_AUTO_BASE_PREMIUM
            case self.INSURANCE_MASTER.INS_HOME:
                self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = (self.INSURANCE_MASTER.INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT / 1000) * self.WS_HOME_RATE_PER_1000
            case self.INSURANCE_MASTER.INS_UMBRELLA:
                self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = self.WS_UMBRELLA_RATE

    def apply_risk_factor(self) -> None:
        """4220-APPLY-RISK-FACTOR.
           IF INS-CLAIMS-COUNT > 2
               COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = WS-CALC-AMOUNT * 1.25
           END-IF.
        """
        if self.INSURANCE_MASTER.INS_CLAIMS_COUNT is not None and self.INSURANCE_MASTER.INS_CLAIMS_COUNT > 2:
            self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT * Decimal('1.25')

    def calculate_final_premium(self) -> None:
        """4230-CALCULATE-FINAL-PREMIUM.
           MOVE WS-CALC-AMOUNT TO INS-PREMIUM-AMOUNT
           ADD WS-CALC-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-PREMIUMS.
        """
        self.INSURANCE_MASTER.INS_PREMIUM_AMOUNT = self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT
        self.WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS += self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT

    def calculate_portfolio_value(self) -> None:
        """5200-CALCULATE-PORTFOLIO-VALUE.
           DISPLAY "CALCULATING PORTFOLIO VALUES..."
           SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
               READ INVESTMENT-MASTER NEXT
                   AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
                   NOT AT END
                       PERFORM 5210-CALCULATE-POSITION-VALUE
                       PERFORM 5220-CALCULATE-GAIN-LOSS
                       PERFORM 5230-UPDATE-TOTALS
               END-READ
           END-PERFORM.
        """
        print("CALCULATING PORTFOLIO VALUES...")
        self.WS_NOT_EOF = True
        while self.WS_NOT_EOF:
            try:
                self.INVESTMENT_MASTER = self.INVESTMENT_MASTER_FILE.pop(0)
                self.calculate_position_value()
                self.calculate_gain_loss()
                self.update_totals()
            except IndexError:
                self.WS_EOF = True
                self.WS_NOT_EOF = False

    def calculate_position_value(self) -> None:
        """5210-CALCULATE-POSITION-VALUE.
           COMPUTE INV-MARKET-VALUE = 
               INV-QUANTITY * INV-CURRENT-PRICE.
        """
        if self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.INV_QUANTITY is not None and self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.INV_CURRENT_PRICE is not None:
            self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.INV_MARKET_VALUE = Decimal(self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.INV_QUANTITY) * self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.INV_CURRENT_PRICE

    def calculate_gain_loss(self) -> None:
        """5220-CALCULATE-GAIN-LOSS.
           COMPUTE INV-GAIN-LOSS = 
               INV-MARKET-VALUE - (INV-QUANTITY * INV-PURCHASE-PRICE).
        """
        if self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.INV_MARKET_VALUE is not None and self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.INV_QUANTITY is not None and self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.INV_PURCHASE_PRICE is not None:
            self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.INV_GAIN_LOSS = self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.INV_MARKET_VALUE - (Decimal(self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.INV_QUANTITY) * self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.INV_PURCHASE_PRICE)

    def update_totals(self) -> None:
        """5230-UPDATE-TOTALS.
           ADD INV-MARKET-VALUE TO WS-TOTAL-INVESTMENTS.

        if self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.INV_MARKET_VALUE is not None:
            self.WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.INV_MARKET_VALUE

           DISPLAY "CALCULATING DIVIDENDS..."
           SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
               READ INVESTMENT-MASTER NEXT

                   NOT AT END

                           PERFORM 5410-COMPUTE-DIVIDEND
                           PERFORM 5420-POST-DIVIDEND

               END-READ
           END-PERFORM.

        self.WS_NOT_EOF = True
        while self.WS_NOT_EOF:

                self.INVESTMENT_MASTER = self.INVESTMENT_MASTER_FILE.pop(0)
                if self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.INV_DIVIDEND_RATE is not None and self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.INV_DIVIDEND_RATE > 0:
                    self.compute_dividend()
                    self.post_dividend()
            except IndexError:
                self.WS_EOF = True
                self.WS_NOT_EOF = False

    def compute_dividend(self) -> None:
        """5410-COMPUTE-DIVIDEND.
           COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = 
               INV-MARKET-VALUE * INV-DIVIDEND-RATE / 4.
        """
        if self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.INV_MARKET_VALUE is not None and self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.INV_DIVIDEND_RATE is not None:
            self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.INV_MARKET_VALUE * self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.INV_DIVIDEND_RATE / 4

        """8300-FORMAT-DATE.
           STRING WS-TEMP-DATE(1:4) DELIMITED SIZE
                  '-' DELIMITED SIZE
                  WS-TEMP-DATE(5:2) DELIMITED SIZE
                  '-' DELIMITED SIZE

    def validate_account(self) -> None:

           SET WS-VALID TO TRUE
           IF ACCT-ID = SPACES
               SET WS-INVALID TO TRUE
           END-IF.
        """
        self.WS_VALID = True
        #ACCOUT_ID = SPACES - Assuming Account ID is a parameter
        #if ACCT_ID == "":
        #    self.WS_INVALID = True

    def calculate_tax(self) -> None:
        """8500-CALCULATE-TAX.
           EVALUATE TRUE
               WHEN WS-CALC-AMOUNT <= WS-BRACKET-1-MAX
                   COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
                       WS-CALC-AMOUNT * WS-BRACKET-1-RATE
               WHEN WS-CALC-AMOUNT <= WS-BRACKET-2-MAX
                   COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
                       (WS-BRACKET-1-MAX * WS-BRACKET-1-RATE) +
                       ((WS-CALC-AMOUNT - WS-BRACKET-1-MAX) * 
                        WS-BRACKET-2-RATE)
               WHEN WS-CALC-AMOUNT <= WS-BRACKET-3-MAX
                   COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
                       (WS-BRACKET-1-MAX * WS-BRACKET-1-RATE) +
                       ((WS-BRACKET-2-MAX - WS-BRACKET-1-MAX) * 
                        WS-BRACKET-2-RATE) +
                       ((WS-CALC-AMOUNT - WS-BRACKET-2-MAX) * 
                        WS-BRACKET-3-RATE)
               WHEN OTHER
                   COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
                       WS-CALC-AMOUNT * WS-BRACKET-5-RATE
           END-EVALUATE.
        """
        if self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= self.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX:
            self.WS_CALC_TAX = self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT * self.WS_BRACKET_1_RATE
        elif self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= self.WS_BRACKET_2_MAX:
            self.WS_CALC_TAX = (self.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * self.WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + \
                                ((self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT - self.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * self.WS_BRACKET_2_RATE)
        elif self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= self.WS_BRACKET_3_MAX:
            self.WS_CALC_TAX = (self.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * self.WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + \
                                ((self.WS_BRACKET_2_MAX - self.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * self.WS_BRACKET_2_RATE) + \
                                ((self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT - self.WS_BRACKET_2_MAX) * self.WS_BRACKET_3_RATE)
        else:
            self.WS_CALC_TAX = self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT * self.WS_BRACKET_5_RATE

    def termination(self) -> None:
        """9000-TERMINATION.
           PERFORM 9100-CLOSE-FILES
           PERFORM 9200-DISPLAY-STATISTICS
           DISPLAY "MEGA-ENTERPRISE SYSTEM TERMINATED NORMALLY".
        """
        self.close_files()
        self.display_statistics()
        print("MEGA-ENTERPRISE SYSTEM TERMINATED NORMALLY")

    def close_files(self) -> None:
        """9100-CLOSE-FILES.
           CLOSE CUSTOMER-MASTER
           CLOSE ACCOUNT-MASTER
           CLOSE LOAN-MASTER
           CLOSE INSURANCE-MASTER
           CLOSE INVESTMENT-MASTER
           CLOSE TRANSACTION-LOG
           CLOSE AUDIT-TRAIL
           CLOSE REPORT-FILE.
        """
        # In Python, closing files is typically handled with context managers.
        # Assuming these are lists that are being used as files, clearing them simulates closing.
        self.CUSTOMER_MASTER_FILE.clear()
        self.ACCOUNT_MASTER_FILE.clear()
        self.LOAN_MASTER_FILE.clear()
        self.INSURANCE_MASTER_FILE.clear()
        self.INVESTMENT_MASTER_FILE.clear()
        self.TRANSACTION_LOG_FILE.clear()
        self.AUDIT_TRAIL_FILE.clear()
        self.REPORT_FILE.clear()

    def display_statistics(self) -> None:
        """9200-DISPLAY-STATISTICS.
           DISPLAY "============================================"
           DISPLAY "       PROCESSING STATISTICS                "
           DISPLAY "============================================"
           MOVE WS-CUST-COUNT TO WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
           DISPLAY "CUSTOMERS PROCESSED:    " WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
           MOVE WS-ACCT-COUNT TO WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
           DISPLAY "ACCOUNTS PROCESSED:     " WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
           MOVE WS-TRAN-COUNT TO WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
           DISPLAY "TRANSACTIONS PROCESSED: " WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
           MOVE WS-LOAN-COUNT TO WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
           DISPLAY "LOANS PROCESSED:        " WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
           MOVE WS-ERROR-COUNT TO WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
           DISPLAY "ERRORS ENCOUNTERED:     " WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
           DISPLAY "============================================"
           MOVE WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS TO WS-FORMATTED-AMOUNT
           DISPLAY "TOTAL DEPOSITS:    " WS-FORMATTED-AMOUNT
           MOVE WS-TOTAL-WITHDRAWALS TO WS-FORMATTED-AMOUNT
           DISPLAY "TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: " WS-FORMATTED-AMOUNT
           MOVE WS-TOTAL-INTEREST TO WS-FORMATTED-AMOUNT
           DISPLAY "TOTAL INTEREST:    " WS-FORMATTED-AMOUNT
           MOVE WS-TOTAL-FEES TO WS-FORMATTED-AMOUNT
           DISPLAY "TOTAL FEES:        " WS-FORMATTED-AMOUNT
           DISPLAY "============================================".
        """
        print("============================================")
        print("       PROCESSING STATISTICS                ")
        print("============================================")
        self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT = str(self.WS_CUST_COUNT)
        print("CUSTOMERS PROCESSED:    " + self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT)
        self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT = str(self.WS_ACCT_COUNT)
        print("ACCOUNTS PROCESSED:     " + self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT)
        self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT = str(self.WS_TRAN_COUNT)
        print("TRANSACTIONS PROCESSED: " + self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT)
        self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT = str(self.WS_LOAN_COUNT)
        print("LOANS PROCESSED:        " + self.WS_FORMATTED

@dataclass
class CustomerMaster:
    CUST_ID: str = ""
    CUST_NAME: str = ""

def customer_segmentation():
    """9310-CUSTOMER-SEGMENTATION."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    print("SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True # SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
    while WS_NOT_EOF:  # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
        try:
            #READ CUSTOMER-MASTER NEXT
            #Simulated READ
            if WS_PROCESS_COUNT > 5: # Simulate EOF
                WS_EOF = True
                WS_NOT_EOF = False
                raise StopIteration
                
            next_customer = CustomerMaster()  # Simulate reading a customer
            WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1
            calculate_clv(next_customer) # PERFORM 9311-CALCULATE-CLV
            assign_segment() # PERFORM 9312-ASSIGN-SEGMENT
        except StopIteration: # AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
            WS_EOF = True
            WS_NOT_EOF = False

def calculate_clv(customer: CustomerMaster):
    """9311-CALCULATE-CLV."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, WS_SAVINGS_RATE, WS_PERSONAL_RATE
    WS_CALC_RESULT = (customer.CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE * WS_SAVINGS_RATE) + \
                     (customer.CUST_TOTAL_LOANS * WS_PERSONAL_RATE) + \
                     (customer.CUST_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS * Decimal("0.01"))  # COMPUTE WS-CALC-RESULT = (CUST-TOTAL-BALANCE * WS-SAVINGS-RATE) + (CUST-TOTAL-LOANS * WS-PERSONAL-RATE) + (CUST-TOTAL-INVESTMENTS * 0.01)

def assign_segment():
    """9312-ASSIGN-SEGMENT."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, WS_TEMP_CODE
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > 10000: # WHEN WS-CALC-RESULT > 10000
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'PLATINUM' # MOVE 'PLATINUM' TO WS-TEMP-CODE
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 5000: # WHEN WS-CALC-RESULT > 5000
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'GOLD' # MOVE 'GOLD' TO WS-TEMP-CODE
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 1000: # WHEN WS-CALC-RESULT > 1000
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'SILVER' # MOVE 'SILVER' TO WS-TEMP-CODE
    else: # WHEN OTHER
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'BRONZE' # MOVE 'BRONZE' TO WS-TEMP-CODE

def sweep_accounts():
    """9632-SWEEP-ACCOUNTS."""
    global ACCT_BALANCE, ACCT_MIN_BALANCE, WS_CALC_AMOUNT, WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS
    if ACCT_BALANCE > ACCT_MIN_BALANCE: # IF ACCT-BALANCE > ACCT-MIN-BALANCE
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = ACCT_BALANCE - ACCT_MIN_BALANCE # COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = ACCT-BALANCE - ACCT-MIN-BALANCE
        ACCT_BALANCE -= WS_CALC_AMOUNT # SUBTRACT WS-CALC-AMOUNT FROM ACCT-BALANCE
        WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += WS_CALC_AMOUNT # ADD WS-CALC-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-INVESTMENTS

from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Optional

@dataclass
class DataRecord:
    """Data Record Structure"""
    CUST_STATUS: str = ""
    CUST_SSN: str = ""
    CUST_CREDIT_SCORE: int = 0
    CUST_RISK_RATING: str = ""
    CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    CUST_TOTAL_LOANS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TEMP_CODE: str = ""
    WS_CALC_RESULT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_CALC_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TOTAL_FEES: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_PROCESS_COUNT: int = 0
    WS_ERROR_COUNT: int = 0
    WS_NOT_EOF: bool = False
    WS_EOF: bool = False
    TRAN_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    TRANSACTION_LOG: str = ""
    WS_CURRENT_TIMESTAMP: str = ""
    WS_TEMP_STRING: str = ""
    WS_VALID: bool = False
    LOAN_CURRENT_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    LOAN_PAID_OFF: bool = False
    WS_ATM_FEE_FOREIGN: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_FORMATTED_COUNT: str = ""
    WS_CUST_COUNT: int = 0

data = DataRecord()

def c100_transaction_monitoring():
    """C100-TRANSACTION-MONITORING."""
    print("MONITORING TRANSACTIONS...") # DISPLAY "MONITORING TRANSACTIONS..."
    data.WS_NOT_EOF = True # SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
    while not data.WS_EOF: # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
        #READ TRANSACTION-LOG NEXT
        # Assuming reading transaction log sets WS_EOF to True at the end
        transaction = read_transaction_log()
        if transaction is None: #AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
            data.WS_EOF = True
        else: #NOT AT END
            data.TRANSACTION_LOG = transaction
            c110_rule_based_detection() #PERFORM C110-RULE-BASED-DETECTION
            c120_behavior_analysis() #PERFORM C120-BEHAVIOR-ANALYSIS
            c130_network_analysis() #PERFORM C130-NETWORK-ANALYSIS

def read_transaction_log() -> Optional[str]:
    """Dummy function to simulate reading from a transaction log."""
    # In real implementation, this would read from a file or database.
    # For simulation purposes, return None after a few calls to simulate EOF.
    global transaction_count
    transaction_count += 1
    if transaction_count > 3:
        return None
    return f"Transaction {transaction_count}"

transaction_count = 0

def c110_rule_based_detection():
    """C110-RULE-BASED-DETECTION."""
    if data.TRAN_AMOUNT >= 10000: # IF TRAN-AMOUNT >= 10000
        c111_flag_ctr() # PERFORM C111-FLAG-CTR
    if 5000 <= data.TRAN_AMOUNT < 10000: # IF TRAN-AMOUNT >= 5000 AND TRAN-AMOUNT < 10000
        c112_check_structuring() # PERFORM C112-CHECK-STRUCTURING

def c111_flag_ctr():
    """C111-FLAG-CTR."""
    data.WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1 # ADD 1 TO WS-PROCESS-COUNT.

def c112_check_structuring():
    """C112-CHECK-STRUCTURING."""
    data.WS_ERROR_COUNT += 1 # ADD 1 TO WS-ERROR-COUNT.

def d110_classification():
    """D110-CLASSIFICATION."""
    if data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE > 750: # IF CUST-CREDIT-SCORE > 750
        data.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'A' # MOVE 'A' TO CUST-RISK-RATING
    elif data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE > 650: # ELSE IF CUST-CREDIT-SCORE > 650
        data.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'B' # MOVE 'B' TO CUST-RISK-RATING
    elif data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE > 550: # ELSE IF CUST-CREDIT-SCORE > 550
        data.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'C' # MOVE 'C' TO CUST-RISK-RATING
    else: # ELSE
        data.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'D' # MOVE 'D' TO CUST-RISK-RATING

def d120_regression():
    """D120-REGRESSION."""
    data.WS_CALC_RESULT = (data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE * 10) + (data.CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE / 1000) - (data.CUST_TOTAL_LOANS / 2000) # COMPUTE WS-CALC-RESULT = (CUST-CREDIT-SCORE * 10) + (CUST-TOTAL-BALANCE / 1000) - (CUST-TOTAL-LOANS / 2000).

        """1300-INITIALIZE-TABLES."""
        for self.WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 101):  # PERFORM VARYING WS-TBL-IDX FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-TBL-IDX > 100
            self.RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[self.WS_TBL_IDX-1] = RateTableEntry() # INITIALIZE RATE-TABLE-ENTRY(WS-TBL-IDX)
            self.RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[self.WS_TBL_IDX-1].RT_RATE = Decimal("0.00") # MOVE ZEROES TO RT-RATE(WS-TBL-IDX)
            self.RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[self.WS_TBL_IDX-1].RT_CODE = "" # MOVE SPACES TO RT-CODE(WS-TBL-IDX)
        for self.WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 51):  # PERFORM VARYING WS-TBL-IDX FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-TBL-IDX > 50
            self.BRANCH_TABLE_ENTRY[self.WS_TBL_IDX - 1] = BranchTableEntry() # INITIALIZE BRANCH-TABLE-ENTRY(WS-TBL-IDX)

    def load_reference_data(self):
        """1400-LOAD-REFERENCE-DATA."""
        self.WS_TBL_IDX = 1  # MOVE 1 TO WS-TBL-IDX
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y' and self.WS_TBL_IDX <= 100:  # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y' OR WS-TBL-IDX > 100
            try:
                line = self.REFERENCE_FILE.readline().strip()
                if not line:  # AT END
                    self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
                else:  # NOT AT END
                    # Assuming the file is comma-separated code,rate
                    code, rate = line.split(",")
                    self.WS_REF_RECORD.WS_REF_CODE = code # MOVE WS-REF-CODE TO RT-CODE(WS-TBL-IDX)
                    self.WS_REF_RECORD.WS_REF_RATE = Decimal(rate) # MOVE WS-REF-RATE TO RT-RATE(WS-TBL-IDX)
                    self.RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[self.WS_TBL_IDX-1].RT_CODE = self.WS_REF_RECORD.WS_REF_CODE
                    self.RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[self.WS_TBL_IDX-1].RT_RATE = self.WS_REF_RECORD.WS_REF_RATE
                    self.WS_TBL_IDX += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-TBL-IDX
            except Exception as e:
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
                print(f"Error reading REFERENCE_FILE: {e}") #Handle file read errors, set EOF
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

    def process_transactions(self):
        """2000-PROCESS-TRANSACTIONS."""
        try:
            line = self.TRANSACTION_FILE.readline().strip()
            if not line:  # AT END
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            else:  # NOT AT END
                # Assuming comma separated transaction file
                txn_account_id, txn_amount, txn_type, txn_target_account = line.split(",")
                self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID = txn_account_id
                self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT = Decimal(txn_amount)
                self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TYPE = txn_type
                self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT = txn_target_account

                self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID
                self.TXN_AMOUNT = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT
                self.TXN_TYPE = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TYPE
                self.TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT

                self.WS_TRANS_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-TRANS-COUNT
                self.validate_transaction()  # PERFORM 2100-VALIDATE-TRANSACTION
                if self.WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':  # IF WS-VALID-FLAG = 'Y'
                    self.process_by_type()  # PERFORM 2200-PROCESS-BY-TYPE
                else:  # ELSE
                    self.handle_error()  # PERFORM 2900-HANDLE-ERROR
        except Exception as e:
            self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y' # Simulate end of file on error
            print(f"Error reading TRANSACTION_FILE: {e}")

    def validate_transaction(self):
        """2100-VALIDATE-TRANSACTION."""
        self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
        if self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID == '' or self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID is None:  # IF TXN-ACCOUNT-ID = SPACES OR LOW-VALUES
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID'  # MOVE 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
            return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH
        try:
            Decimal(self.TXN_AMOUNT) # Check it converts OK
        except:
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID AMOUNT'  # MOVE 'INVALID AMOUNT' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
            return
        #IF TXN-AMOUNT IS NOT NUMERIC
        valid_types = ['D', 'W', 'T', 'I']
        if self.TXN_TYPE not in valid_types: #IF TXN-TYPE NOT = 'D' AND TXN-TYPE NOT = 'W' AND TXN-TYPE NOT = 'T' AND TXN-TYPE NOT = 'I'
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE'  # MOVE 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
        self.validate_account_exists()  # PERFORM 2150-VALIDATE-ACCOUNT-EXISTS
        self.validate_business_rules()  # PERFORM 2160-VALIDATE-BUSINESS-RULES

    def validate_account_exists(self):
        """2150-VALIDATE-ACCOUNT-EXISTS."""
        self.WS_SEARCH_KEY = self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID  # MOVE TXN-ACCOUNT-ID TO WS-SEARCH-KEY
        self.search_account()  # PERFORM 5000-SEARCH-ACCOUNT
        if self.WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'N':  # IF WS-FOUND-FLAG = 'N'
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'  # MOVE 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND' TO WS-ERROR-MSG

    def validate_business_rules(self):
        """2160-VALIDATE-BUSINESS-RULES."""
        if self.TXN_TYPE == 'W':  # IF TXN-TYPE = 'W'
            if self.TXN_AMOUNT > self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:  # IF TXN-AMOUNT > WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
                self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
                self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'  # MOVE 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
        if self.TXN_AMOUNT > Decimal("1000000.00"):  # IF TXN-AMOUNT > 1000000
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT'  # MOVE 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT' TO WS-ERROR-MSG

    def process_by_type(self):
        """2200-PROCESS-BY-TYPE."""
        match self.TXN_TYPE: #EVALUATE TXN-TYPE
            case 'D': #WHEN 'D'
                self.process_deposit()  # PERFORM 2300-PROCESS-DEPOSIT
            case 'W': #WHEN 'W'
                self.process_withdrawal()  # PERFORM 2400-PROCESS-WITHDRAWAL
            case 'T': #WHEN 'T'
                self.process_transfer()  # PERFORM 2500-PROCESS-TRANSFER
            case 'I': #WHEN 'I'
                self.process_interest()  # PERFORM 2600-PROCESS-INTEREST
            case _: #WHEN OTHER
                self.handle_error()  # PERFORM 2900-HANDLE-ERROR

    def process_deposit(self):
        """2300-PROCESS-DEPOSIT."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += self.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        self.WS_TXN_DESC = 'DEPOSIT'  # MOVE 'DEPOSIT' TO WS-TXN-DESC
        self.WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS += self.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS
        self.WS_DEPOSIT_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-DEPOSIT-COUNT
        self.update_account()  # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
        self.write_audit_trail()  # PERFORM 2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL

    def update_account(self):
        """2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT."""
        self.ACCT_BALANCE = self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE  # MOVE WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE TO ACCT-BALANCE
        #self.ACCT_LAST_UPDATE = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")  # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO ACCT-LAST-UPDATE
        self.ACCT_LAST_UPDATE = "20240101"
        self.rewrite_account_record()  # REWRITE ACCOUNT-RECORD
        if self.WS_FILE_STATUS != '00':  # IF WS-FILE-STATUS NOT = '00'
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'UPDATE FAILED'  # MOVE 'UPDATE FAILED' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
            self.handle_error()  # PERFORM 2900-HANDLE-ERROR

    def rewrite_account_record(self):
        """Simulates rewriting the account record in a file."""
        # In a real system, this would involve writing the updated record
        # back to the MASTER_FILE.  Since we don't have a real file, we'll
        # just simulate it.
        pass

    def write_audit_trail(self):
        """2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL."""
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD = AuditRecord()  # INITIALIZE WS-AUDIT-RECORD
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.AUDIT_ACCOUNT = self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID  # MOVE TXN-ACCOUNT-ID TO AUDIT-ACCOUNT
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.AUDIT_AMOUNT = self.TXN_AMOUNT  # MOVE TXN-AMOUNT TO AUDIT-AMOUNT
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.AUDIT_TYPE = self.TXN_TYPE  # MOVE TXN-TYPE TO AUDIT-TYPE
        #self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.AUDIT_TIMESTAMP = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d%H%M%S")  # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO AUDIT-TIMESTAMP
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.AUDIT_TIMESTAMP = "20240101000000"
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.AUDIT_JOB_ID = self.WS_JOB_ID  # MOVE WS-JOB-ID TO AUDIT-JOB-ID
        self.write_audit_record()  # WRITE AUDIT-RECORD FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD

    def write_audit_record(self):
        """Simulates writing the audit record to a file."""
        # In a real system, this would involve writing the WS_AUDIT_RECORD
        # to the AUDIT_FILE.  Since we don't have a real file, we'll
        # just simulate it.
        pass

    def process_withdrawal(self):
        """2400-PROCESS-WITHDRAWAL."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.TXN_AMOUNT  # SUBTRACT TXN-AMOUNT FROM WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        self.WS_TXN_DESC = 'WITHDRAWAL'  # MOVE 'WITHDRAWAL' TO WS-TXN-DESC
        self.WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS += self.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-WITHDRAWALS
        self.WS_WITHDRAWAL_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-WITHDRAWAL-COUNT
        self.update_account()  # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
        self.write_audit_trail()  # PERFORM 2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL
        if self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE < self.WS_MIN_BALANCE_LIMIT:  # IF WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE < WS-MIN-BALANCE-LIMIT
            self.generate_low_balance_alert()  # PERFORM 2450-GENERATE-LOW-BALANCE-ALERT

    def generate_low_balance_alert(self):
        """2450-GENERATE-LOW-BALANCE-ALERT."""
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD = AlertRecord()  # INITIALIZE WS-ALERT-RECORD
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD.ALERT_TYPE = 'LOW-BAL'  # MOVE 'LOW-BAL' TO ALERT-TYPE
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD.ALERT_ACCOUNT = self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID  # MOVE TXN-ACCOUNT-ID TO ALERT-ACCOUNT
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD.ALERT_BALANCE = self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE  # MOVE WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE TO ALERT-BALANCE
        #self.WS_ALERT_RECORD.ALERT_DATE = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")  # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO ALERT-DATE
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD.ALERT_DATE = "20240101"
        self.write_alert_record()  # WRITE ALERT-RECORD FROM WS-ALERT-RECORD
        self.WS_ALERT_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-ALERT-COUNT

    def write_alert_record(self):
        """Simulates writing an alert record."""
        pass

    def process_transfer(self):
        """2500-PROCESS-TRANSFER."""
        self.validate_target_account()  # PERFORM 2510-VALIDATE-TARGET-ACCOUNT
        if self.WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':  # IF WS-VALID-FLAG = 'Y'
            self.debit_source()  # PERFORM 2520-DEBIT-SOURCE
            self.credit_target()  # PERFORM 2530-CREDIT-TARGET
            self.record_transfer()  # PERFORM 2540-RECORD-TRANSFER
        else:  # ELSE
            self.handle_error()  # PERFORM 2900-HANDLE-ERROR

    def validate_target_account(self):
        """2510-VALIDATE-TARGET-ACCOUNT."""
        self.WS_SEARCH_KEY = self.TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT  # MOVE TXN-TARGET-ACCOUNT TO WS-SEARCH-KEY
        self.search_account()  # PERFORM 5000-SEARCH-ACCOUNT
        if self.WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'N':  # IF WS-FOUND-FLAG = 'N'
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'TARGET ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'  # MOVE 'TARGET ACCOUNT NOT FOUND' TO WS-ERROR-MSG

    def debit_source(self):
        """2520-DEBIT-SOURCE."""
        self.WS_SOURCE_BALANCE -= self.TXN_AMOUNT  # SUBTRACT TXN-AMOUNT FROM WS-SOURCE-BALANCE
        self.ACCT_BALANCE = self.WS_SOURCE_BALANCE  # MOVE WS-SOURCE-BALANCE TO ACCT-BALANCE
        self.rewrite_account_record()  # REWRITE ACCOUNT-RECORD

    def credit_target(self):
        """2530-CREDIT-TARGET."""
        self.WS_TARGET_BALANCE += self.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-TARGET-BALANCE
        self.ACCT_ID = self.TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT  # MOVE TXN-TARGET-ACCOUNT TO ACCT-ID
        self.read_master_file()  # READ MASTER-FILE INTO WS-ACCOUNT-REC
        self.ACCT_BALANCE = self.WS_TARGET_BALANCE  # MOVE WS-TARGET-BALANCE TO ACCT-BALANCE
        self.rewrite_account_record()  # REWRITE ACCOUNT-RECORD

    def read_master_file(self):
        """Simulates reading the master file.
        This is a placeholder.  In a real COBOL system, this would read a record
        from the MASTER-FILE based on ACCT-ID. We don't have an actual master file,
        so we'll just leave the WS-ACCOUNT-REC as is.
        """
        pass

    def record_transfer(self):
        """2540-RECORD-TRANSFER."""
        self.WS_TOTAL_TRANSFERS += self.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-TRANSFERS
        self.WS_TRANSFER_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS

        self.WS_CURRENT_SHARES: int = 0
        self.WS_ROUTING_TYPE: str = ""
        self.WS_ORDER_TIME: int = 0
        self.ORDER_MARKET: bool = False
        self.ORDER_LIMIT: bool = False
        self.ORDER_STOP: bool = False
        self.WS_CURRENT_MARKET_PRICE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_EXECUTED_PRICE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_TRADE_STATUS: str = ""
        self.WS_EXECUTION_TIME: int = 0
        self.WS_STOP_PRICE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_GROSS_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_COMMISSION: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_FEES: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_NET_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_NEW_TOTAL_SHARES: int = 0
        self.WS_NEW_COST: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_REALIZED_GAIN: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_TRADE_ID: str = ""
        self.WS_TRADE_RECORD = TradeRecord()
        self.WS_REJECT_RECORD = RejectRecord()
        self.TRADE_RECORD = TradeRecord()
        self.REJECT_RECORD = RejectRecord()
        self.ESTIMATED_PRICE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

        # Insurance Processing Variables
        self.WS_VALID_FLAG: str = ""
        self.WS_ERROR_MSG: str = ""
        self.WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_EFFECTIVE_DATE: int = 0
        self.POLICY_LIFE: bool = False
        self.POLICY_AUTO: bool = False
        self.POLICY_HOME: bool = False
        self.POLICY_HEALTH: bool = False
        self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_INSURED_AGE: int = 0
        self.WS_SMOKER_FLAG: str = ""
        self.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_VEHICLE_AGE: int = 0


    def calculate_pmi(self) -> None:
        """10335-CALCULATE-PMI."""
        match True:
            case self.WS_LTV_RATIO > 95:
                self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.0125") / 12
            case self.WS_LTV_RATIO > 90:
                self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.0100") / 12
            case self.WS_LTV_RATIO > 85:
                self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.0075") / 12
            case _:
                self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.0050") / 12

    def evaluate_history(self) -> None:
        """10340-EVALUATE-HISTORY."""
        if self.WS_LATE_90_DAYS > 0:
            self.WS_RISK_SCORE -= 50
            self.WS_FACTOR_1 = 'SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY'
        if self.WS_LATE_60_DAYS > 2:
            self.WS_RISK_SCORE -= 30
            self.WS_FACTOR_2 = '60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES'
        if self.WS_LATE_30_DAYS > 5:
            self.WS_RISK_SCORE -= 20
            self.WS_FACTOR_3 = 'MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES'

    def calculate_final_risk(self) -> None:
        """10350-CALCULATE-FINAL-RISK."""
        self.WS_RISK_SCORE = self.WS_RISK_SCORE / 4
        match True:
            case self.WS_RISK_SCORE >= 80:
                self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'LOW RISK'
            case self.WS_RISK_SCORE >= 60:
                self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'MODERATE'
            case self.WS_RISK_SCORE >= 40:
                self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'ELEVATED'
            case _:
                self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'HIGH RISK'

    def determine_approval(self) -> None:
        """10400-DETERMINE-APPROVAL."""
        if self.WS_CREDIT_TIER == 'F':
            self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'D'
            self.WS_CONDITIONS = 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW'
            return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH
        if self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY == 'HIGH RISK':
            self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'D'
            self.WS_CONDITIONS = 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED'
            return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH
        if self.WS_DTI_RATIO > 50:
            self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'D'
            self.WS_CONDITIONS = 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH'
            return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH
        self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'A'
        self.calculate_approved_terms()

    def calculate_approved_terms(self) -> None:
        """10450-CALCULATE-APPROVED-TERMS."""
        self.WS_APPROVED_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
        match self.WS_CREDIT_TIER:
            case 'A':
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal("0.00")
            case 'B':
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal("0.50")
            case 'C':
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal("1.50")
            case 'D':
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal("3.00")
        if self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY == 'ELEVATED':
            self.WS_APPROVED_RATE += Decimal("0.50")

    def generate_loan_terms(self) -> None:
        """10500-GENERATE-LOAN-TERMS."""
        self.WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE = self.WS_APPROVED_RATE
        self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE = self.WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE / 1200
        self.WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR = (1 + self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE) ** self.WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS
        self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT = (
            self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
            * self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE
            * self.WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR
            / (self.WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR - 1)
        )
        self.WS_LOAN_PRINCIPAL_BAL = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT

    def create_amortization(self) -> None:
        """10600-CREATE-AMORTIZATION."""
        self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
        self.WS_PAYMENT_DATE = int(date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")) # Simulate CURRENT-DATE
        for self.WS_AMORT_IDX in range(1, self.WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS + 1):
            self.calculate_payment_split()

    def calculate_payment_split(self) -> None:
        """10650-CALCULATE-PAYMENT-SPLIT."""
        self.AMORT_INTEREST[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE * self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE
        self.AMORT_PRINCIPAL[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = (
            self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT - self.AMORT_INTEREST[self.WS_AMORT_IDX]
        )
        self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE -= self.AMORT_PRINCIPAL[self.WS_AMORT_IDX]
        self.AMORT_BALANCE[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE
        self.AMORT_PAYMENT_NUM[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = self.WS_AMORT_IDX
        self.AMORT_PAYMENT_AMT[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT
        if self.LOAN_MORTGAGE:
            self.AMORT_ESCROW[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = (
                self.WS_PROPERTY_TAX + self.WS_INSURANCE_PREMIUM
            ) / 12
            self.AMORT_TOTAL_PMT[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = (
                self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT
                + self.AMORT_ESCROW[self.WS_AMORT_IDX]
                + self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT
            )
        else:
            self.AMORT_TOTAL_PMT[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT
        self.advance_payment_date()

    def advance_payment_date(self) -> None:
        """10660-ADVANCE-PAYMENT-DATE."""
        self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH += 1
        if self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH > 12:
            self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH = 1
            self.WS_PAYMENT_YEAR += 1
        self.AMORT_PAYMENT_DATE[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = (
            self.WS_PAYMENT_YEAR * 10000 + self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH * 100 + 1
        )

    def finalize_loan(self) -> None:
        """10700-FINALIZE-LOAN."""
        self.WS_LOAN_START_DATE = int(date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")) # Simulate CURRENT-DATE
        self.WS_LOAN_END_DATE = self.WS_LOAN_START_DATE + (self.WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS * 30)
        self.WS_LOAN_STATUS = 'A'
        self.create_loan_record()
        self.disburse_funds()
        self.send_confirmation()

    def create_loan_record(self) -> None:
        """10750-CREATE-LOAN-RECORD."""
        self.WS_LOAN_RECORD = LoanRecord()
        self.LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_id = self.WS_LOAN_ID
        self.LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_type = self.WS_LOAN_TYPE
        self.LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_amount = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
        self.LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_rate = self.WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE
        self.LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_payment = self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT
        self.LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_start = self.WS_LOAN_START_DATE
        self.LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_status = self.WS_LOAN_STATUS
        # Assume WRITE is a no-op for now
        #WRITE LOAN-RECORD FROM WS-LOAN-RECORD.

    def disburse_funds(self) -> None:
        """10760-DISBURSE-FUNDS."""
        self.WS_DISBURSEMENT_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
        self.process_deposit()
        self.write_audit_trail()

    def send_confirmation(self) -> None:
        """10770-SEND-CONFIRMATION."""
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'LOAN-CONFIRM'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Your loan has been approved'
        self.send_notification()

    def process_decline(self) -> None:
        """10800-PROCESS-DECLINE."""
        self.WS_LOAN_STATUS = 'DECLINED'
        self.record_decline()
        self.send_decline_notice()

    def record_decline(self) -> None:
        """10810-RECORD-DECLINE."""
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD = DeclineRecord()
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_loan_id = self.WS_LOAN_ID
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_status = self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_reason = self.WS_CONDITIONS
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_date = int(date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")) # Simulate CURRENT-DATE
        # Assume WRITE is a no-op for now
        #WRITE DECLINE-RECORD FROM WS-DECLINE-RECORD.

    def send_decline_notice(self) -> None:
        """10820-SEND-DECLINE-NOTICE."""
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'LOAN-DECLINE'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'LETTER'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Regarding your loan application'
        self.send_notification()

    def portfolio_management(self) -> None:
        """11000-PORTFOLIO-MANAGEMENT."""
        self.load_portfolio()
        self.update_market_prices()
        self.calculate_values()
        self.rebalance_check()
        self.generate_statements()

    def load_portfolio(self) -> None:
        """11100-LOAD-PORTFOLIO."""
        self.WS_HOLD_IDX = 1
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = ''

        # Simulate reading from file (replace with actual file reading logic)
        holdings_data = [
            HoldingRec(hold_symbol="AAPL", hold_shares=100, hold_cost_per_share=Decimal("150.00"), hold_current_price=Decimal("170.00"), hold_type='STK'),
            HoldingRec(hold_symbol="GOOG", hold_shares=50, hold_cost_per_share=Decimal("2500.00"), hold_current_price=Decimal("2700.00"), hold_type='STK'),
            HoldingRec(hold_symbol="MSFT", hold_shares=75, hold_cost_per_share=Decimal("300.00"), hold_current_price=Decimal("320.00"), hold_type='STK'),
            HoldingRec(hold_symbol="VANGUARD", hold_shares=200, hold_cost_per_share=Decimal("100.00"), hold_current_price=Decimal("105.00"), hold_type='BND')
        ] # example data. Replace with your actual file reading logic


        for holding in holdings_data:
            if self.WS_HOLD_IDX > 100 or self.WS_EOF_FLAG == 'Y':
                break
            self.WS_HOLDING_REC = holding
            self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX] = self.WS_HOLDING_REC
            self.WS_HOLD_IDX += 1
        else:
            self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

        self.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT = self.WS_HOLD_IDX - 1

    def update_market_prices(self) -> None:
        """11200-UPDATE-MARKET-PRICES."""
        for self.WS_HOLD_IDX in range(1, self.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT + 1):
            self.WS_QUOTE_SYMBOL = self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_symbol
            self.get_quote()
            self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_current_price = self.WS_QUOTE_PRICE

    def get_quote(self) -> None:
        """11250-GET-QUOTE."""
        self.QUOTE_REQUEST.quote_request_symbol = self.WS_QUOTE_SYMBOL
        #CALL 'GETQUOTE' USING QUOTE-REQUEST QUOTE-RESPONSE
        # Simulate getting quote (replace with actual API call)
        if self.QUOTE_REQUEST.quote_request_symbol == "AAPL":
            self.QUOTE_RESPONSE.quote_last_price = Decimal("175.00")
            self.QUOTE_RESPONSE.quote_response_status = 'OK'
        elif self.QUOTE_REQUEST.quote_request_symbol == "GOOG":
            self.QUOTE_RESPONSE.quote_last_price = Decimal("2750.00")
            self.QUOTE_RESPONSE.quote_response_status = 'OK'
        elif self.QUOTE_REQUEST.quote_request_symbol == "MSFT":
            self.QUOTE_RESPONSE.quote_last_price = Decimal("325.00")
            self.QUOTE_RESPONSE.quote_response_status = 'OK'
        elif self.QUOTE_REQUEST.quote_request_symbol == "VANGUARD":
            self.QUOTE_RESPONSE.quote_last_price = Decimal("106.00")
            self.QUOTE_RESPONSE.quote_response_status = 'OK'
        else:
            self.QUOTE_RESPONSE.quote_last_price = Decimal("0.00")
            self.QUOTE_RESPONSE.quote_response_status = 'ERROR'

        if self.QUOTE_RESPONSE.quote_response_status == 'OK':
            self.WS_QUOTE_PRICE = self.QUOTE_RESPONSE.quote_last_price
        else:
            self.WS_QUOTE_PRICE = Decimal("0.00")

    def calculate_values(self) -> None:
        """11300-CALCULATE-VALUES."""
        self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_COST_BASIS = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_UNREALIZED_GAIN = Decimal("0.00")
        for self.WS_HOLD_IDX in range(1, self.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT + 1):
            self.calculate_holding_value()

    def calculate_holding_value(self) -> None:
        """11350-CALCULATE-HOLDING-VALUE."""
        self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_market_value = (
            self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_shares
            * self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_current_price
        )
        self.WS_HOLD_COST = (
            self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_shares
            * self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_cost_per_share
        )
        self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_gain_loss = (
            self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_market_value - self.WS_HOLD_COST
        )
        if self.WS_HOLD_COST > 0:
            self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_pct_change = (
                self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_gain_loss / self.WS_HOLD_COST
            ) * 100
        else:
            self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_pct_change = Decimal("0.00")

        self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE += self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_market_value
        self.WS_COST_BASIS += self.WS_HOLD_COST
        self.WS_UNREALIZED_GAIN += self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_gain_loss

    def rebalance_check(self) -> None:
        """11400-REBALANCE-CHECK."""
        self.calculate_current_allocation()
        self.compare_to_target()
        if self.WS_REBALANCE_NEEDED == 'Y':
            self.generate_rebalance_trades()

    def calculate_current_allocation(self) -> None:
        """11410-CALCULATE-CURRENT-ALLOCATION."""
        self.WS_STOCKS_VALUE = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_BONDS_VALUE = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_CASH_VALUE = Decimal("0.00")
        for self.WS_HOLD_IDX in range(1, self.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT + 1):
            match self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_type:
                case 'STK':
                    self.WS_STOCKS_VALUE += self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_market_value
                case 'BND':
                    self.WS_BONDS_VALUE += self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_market_value
                case 'CSH':
                    self.WS_CASH_VALUE += self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_market_value

        self.WS_STOCKS_PCT = (self.WS_STOCKS_VALUE / self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE) * 100
        self.WS_BONDS_PCT = (self.WS_BONDS_VALUE / self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE) * 100
        self.WS_CASH_PCT = (self.WS_CASH_VALUE / self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE) * 100

    def compare_to_target(self) -> None:
        """11420-COMPARE-TO-TARGET."""
        self.WS_REBALANCE_NEEDED = 'N'

def calculate_driver_premium():
    """
    9100-CALC-DRIVER-PREMIUM.
    """
    global WS_BASE_PREMIUM, WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM, WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM, WS_DRIVER_AGE, WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR, WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE, WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR, WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE
    match WS_DRIVER_AGE:
        case age if 6 <= age <= 10:
            # WHEN 6 THRU 10
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM += Decimal("100.00")
        case _:
            # WHEN OTHER
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM += Decimal("50.00")

    if WS_DRIVER_AGE < 25:
        # IF WS-DRIVER-AGE < 25
        WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5")

    if WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR > 0:
        # IF WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR > 0
        WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE = Decimal(WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR * 200)
        WS_BASE_PREMIUM += WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE

    if WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR > 0:
        # IF WS-VIOLATIONS-3YR > 0
        WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE = Decimal(WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR * 100)
        WS_BASE_PREMIUM += WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE

    WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = WS_BASE_PREMIUM
    # MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM
    WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM / Decimal("12.00")
    # COMPUTE WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM = WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM / 12.


def calculate_home_premium():
    """
    13230-CALC-HOME-PREMIUM.
    """
    global WS_BASE_PREMIUM, WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM, WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM, WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT, WS_HOME_AGE, WS_FLOOD_ZONE, WS_SECURITY_SYSTEM, WS_DEDUCTIBLE, WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT
    WS_BASE_PREMIUM = WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.003")
    # COMPUTE WS-BASE-PREMIUM = WS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT * 0.003
    match WS_HOME_AGE:
        case age if 0 <= age <= 10:
            # WHEN 0 THRU 10
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9")
        case age if 11 <= age <= 25:
            # WHEN 11 THRU 25
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0")
        case age if 26 <= age <= 50:
            # WHEN 26 THRU 50
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.2")
        case _:
            # WHEN OTHER
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5")

    if WS_FLOOD_ZONE == 'Y':
        # IF WS-FLOOD-ZONE = 'Y'
        WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5")

    if WS_SECURITY_SYSTEM == 'Y':
        # IF WS-SECURITY-SYSTEM = 'Y'
        WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9")

    WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT = WS_DEDUCTIBLE / Decimal("1000.00") * Decimal("50.00")
    # COMPUTE WS-DEDUCTIBLE-CREDIT = WS-DEDUCTIBLE / 1000 * 50
    WS_BASE_PREMIUM -= WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT
    # SUBTRACT WS-DEDUCTIBLE-CREDIT FROM WS-BASE-PREMIUM

    if WS_BASE_PREMIUM < 200:
        # IF WS-BASE-PREMIUM < 200
        WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal("200.00")

    WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = WS_BASE_PREMIUM
    # MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM
    WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM / Decimal("12.00")
    # COMPUTE WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM = WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM / 12.


def calculate_health_premium():
    """
    13240-CALC-HEALTH-PREMIUM.
    """
    global WS_BASE_PREMIUM, WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM, WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM, WS_INSURED_AGE, WS_PLAN_TYPE, WS_FAMILY_PLAN

    WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal("300.00")
    # MOVE 300 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM

    match WS_INSURED_AGE:
        case age if 0 <= age <= 18:
            # WHEN 0 THRU 18
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.5")
        case age if 19 <= age <= 30:
            # WHEN 19 THRU 30
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0")
        case age if 31 <= age <= 40:
            # WHEN 31 THRU 40
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.3")
        case age if 41 <= age <= 50:
            # WHEN 41 THRU 50
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.6")
        case age if 51 <= age <= 60:
            # WHEN 51 THRU 60
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.0")
        case _:
            # WHEN OTHER
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.8")

    match WS_PLAN_TYPE:
        case 'BRONZE':
            # WHEN 'BRONZE'
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.8")
        case 'SILVER':
            # WHEN 'SILVER'
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0")
        case 'GOLD':
            # WHEN 'GOLD'
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.3")
        case 'PLATINUM':
            # WHEN 'PLATINUM'
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.6")

    if WS_FAMILY_PLAN == 'Y':
        # IF WS-FAMILY-PLAN = 'Y'
        WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.5")

    WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = WS_BASE_PREMIUM
    # MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM
    WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM * Decimal("12.00")
    # COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM = WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM * 12.

def underwriting():
    """
    13300-UNDERWRITING.
    """
    evaluate_risk_factors()
    # PERFORM 13310-EVALUATE-RISK-FACTORS
    check_medical_history()
    # PERFORM 13320-CHECK-MEDICAL-HISTORY
    verify_information()
    # PERFORM 13330-VERIFY-INFORMATION
    determine_decision()
    # PERFORM 13340-DETERMINE-DECISION.

def evaluate_risk_factors():
    """
    13310-EVALUATE-RISK-FACTORS.
    """
    global WS_RISK_POINTS, POLICY_LIFE, WS_BMI, WS_SMOKER_FLAG, WS_HAZARDOUS_OCCUPATION, POLICY_AUTO, WS_DRIVER_AGE, WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR

    WS_RISK_POINTS = 0
    # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RISK-POINTS

    if POLICY_LIFE:
        # IF POLICY-LIFE
        if WS_BMI > 30:
            # IF WS-BMI > 30
            WS_RISK_POINTS += 10
            # ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS

        if WS_SMOKER_FLAG == 'Y':
            # IF WS-SMOKER-FLAG = 'Y'
            WS_RISK_POINTS += 25
            # ADD 25 TO WS-RISK-POINTS

        if WS_HAZARDOUS_OCCUPATION == 'Y':
            # IF WS-HAZARDOUS-OCCUPATION = 'Y'
            WS_RISK_POINTS += 15
            # ADD 15 TO WS-RISK-POINTS

    if POLICY_AUTO:
        # IF POLICY-AUTO
        if WS_DRIVER_AGE < 21:
            # IF WS-DRIVER-AGE < 21
            WS_RISK_POINTS += 20
            # ADD 20 TO WS-RISK-POINTS

        if WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR > 1:
            # IF WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR > 1
            WS_RISK_POINTS += 15
            # ADD 15 TO WS-RISK-POINTS

def check_medical_history():
    """
    13320-CHECK-MEDICAL-HISTORY.
    """
    global WS_RISK_POINTS, WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS, WS_CONDITION_POINTS, WS_RECENT_HOSPITALIZATION, WS_PRESCRIPTION_COUNT

    if WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS > 0:
        # IF WS-CHRONIC-CONDITIONS > 0
        WS_CONDITION_POINTS = Decimal(WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS * 5)
        # COMPUTE WS-CONDITION-POINTS = WS-CHRONIC-CONDITIONS * 5
        WS_RISK_POINTS += int(WS_CONDITION_POINTS)
        # ADD WS-CONDITION-POINTS TO WS-RISK-POINTS

    if WS_RECENT_HOSPITALIZATION == 'Y':
        # IF WS-RECENT-HOSPITALIZATION = 'Y'
        WS_RISK_POINTS += 10
        # ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS

    if WS_PRESCRIPTION_COUNT > 5:
        # IF WS-PRESCRIPTION-COUNT > 5
        WS_RISK_POINTS += 5
        # ADD 5 TO WS-RISK-POINTS

def verify_information():
    """
    13330-VERIFY-INFORMATION.
    """
    check_fraud_indicators()
    # PERFORM 13335-CHECK-FRAUD-INDICATORS
    validate_documents()
    # PERFORM 13336-VALIDATE-DOCUMENTS.

def check_fraud_indicators():
    """
    13335-CHECK-FRAUD-INDICATORS.
    """
    global WS_RISK_POINTS, WS_RECENT_CLAIMS, WS_FRAUD_FLAG, WS_ADDRESS_MISMATCH

    if WS_RECENT_CLAIMS > 3:
        # IF WS-RECENT-CLAIMS > 3
        WS_RISK_POINTS += 20
        # ADD 20 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        WS_FRAUD_FLAG = 'Y'
        # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-FRAUD-FLAG

    if WS_ADDRESS_MISMATCH == 'Y':
        # IF WS-ADDRESS-MISMATCH = 'Y'
        WS_RISK_POINTS += 10
        # ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS

def validate_documents():
    """
    13336-VALIDATE-DOCUMENTS.
    """
    global WS_DOC_MISSING, WS_UW_STATUS

    if WS_DOC_MISSING == 'Y':
        # IF WS-DOC-MISSING = 'Y'
        WS_UW_STATUS = 'PENDING'
        # MOVE 'PENDING' TO WS-UW-STATUS
    else:
        # ELSE
        WS_UW_STATUS = 'COMPLETE'
        # MOVE 'COMPLETE' TO WS-UW-STATUS

def determine_decision():
    """
    13340-DETERMINE-DECISION.
    """
    global WS_RISK_POINTS, WS_UW_DECISION, WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM

    match True:
        case _ if WS_RISK_POINTS > 50:
            # WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 50
            WS_UW_DECISION = 'DECLINE'
            # MOVE 'DECLINE' TO WS-UW-DECISION
        case _ if WS_RISK_POINTS > 30:
            # WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 30
            WS_UW_DECISION = 'SUBSTANDARD'
            # MOVE 'SUBSTANDARD' TO WS-UW-DECISION
            WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5")
            # COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM = WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM * 1.5
        case _ if WS_RISK_POINTS > 15:
            # WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 15
            WS_UW_DECISION = 'STANDARD'
            # MOVE 'STANDARD' TO WS-UW-DECISION
        case _:
            # WHEN OTHER
            WS_UW_DECISION = 'PREFERRED'
            # MOVE 'PREFERRED' TO WS-UW-DECISION
            WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9")
            # COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM = WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM * 0.9

def issue_policy():
    """
    13400-ISSUE-POLICY.
    """
    global WS_UW_DECISION

    if WS_UW_DECISION != 'DECLINE':
        # IF WS-UW-DECISION NOT = 'DECLINE'
        generate_policy_number()
        # PERFORM 13410-GENERATE-POLICY-NUMBER
        create_policy_record()
        # PERFORM 13420-CREATE-POLICY-RECORD
        set_beneficiaries()
        # PERFORM 13430-SET-BENEFICIARIES
        send_policy_docs()
        # PERFORM 13440-SEND-POLICY-DOCS
    else:
        # ELSE
        send_decline_letter()
        # PERFORM 13450-SEND-DECLINE-LETTER

def generate_policy_number():
    """
    13410-GENERATE-POLICY-NUMBER.
    """
    global WS_DATE_PART, WS_POLICY_TYPE, WS_TYPE_PART, WS_RANDOM_PART, WS_POLICY_NUMBER

    WS_DATE_PART = str(date.today())
    # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-DATE-PART
    WS_TYPE_PART = WS_POLICY_TYPE
    # MOVE WS-POLICY-TYPE TO WS-TYPE-PART
    WS_RANDOM_PART = int(random.random() * 99999)
    # COMPUTE WS-RANDOM-PART = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999
    WS_POLICY_NUMBER = f"{WS_TYPE_PART}{WS_DATE_PART}{WS_RANDOM_PART}"
    # STRING WS-TYPE-PART DELIMITED SIZE WS-DATE-PART DELIMITED SIZE WS-RANDOM-PART DELIMITED SIZE INTO WS-POLICY-NUMBER.

def create_policy_record():
    """
    13420-CREATE-POLICY-RECORD.
    """
    global POLICY_RECORD, WS_POLICY_NUMBER, WS_POLICY_TYPE, WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT, WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM, WS_EFFECTIVE_DATE, WS_EXPIRATION_DATE

    POLICY_RECORD = PolicyRecord()
    # INITIALIZE WS-POLICY-RECORD
    POLICY_RECORD.POLICY_REC_NUMBER = WS_POLICY_NUMBER
    # MOVE WS-POLICY-NUMBER TO POLICY-REC-NUMBER
    POLICY_RECORD.POLICY_REC_TYPE = WS_POLICY_TYPE
    # MOVE WS-POLICY-TYPE TO POLICY-REC-TYPE
    POLICY_RECORD.POLICY_REC_COVERAGE = WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT
    # MOVE WS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT TO POLICY-REC-COVERAGE
    POLICY_RECORD.POLICY_REC_PREMIUM = WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM
    # MOVE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM TO POLICY-REC-PREMIUM
    POLICY_RECORD.POLICY_REC_EFF_DATE = WS_EFFECTIVE_DATE
    # MOVE WS-EFFECTIVE-DATE TO POLICY-REC-EFF-DATE
    POLICY_RECORD.POLICY_REC_EXP_DATE = WS_EXPIRATION_DATE
    # MOVE WS-EXPIRATION-DATE TO POLICY-REC-EXP-DATE
    POLICY_RECORD.POLICY_REC_STATUS = 'A'
    # MOVE 'A' TO POLICY-REC-STATUS
    # WRITE POLICY-RECORD FROM WS-POLICY-RECORD.
    write_policy_record(POLICY_RECORD)

def write_policy_record(policy_record: PolicyRecord):
    """
    Simulates writing the policy record.
    """
    print(f"Writing policy record: {policy_record}")

def set_beneficiaries():
    """
    13430-SET-BENEFICIARIES.
    """
    global WS_BENEF_IDX, BENEF_NAME, BENEF_RELATION, BENEF_PCT, WS_POLICY_NUMBER, BENEFICIARY_RECORD
    WS_BENEF_IDX = 1
    while WS_BENEF_IDX <= 5:
        # PERFORM VARYING WS-BENEF-IDX FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-BENEF-IDX > 5
        if BENEF_NAME[WS_BENEF_IDX] != "":  # Assuming SPACES is an empty string
            # IF BENEF-NAME(WS-BENEF-IDX) NOT = SPACES
            beneficiary_rec = BeneficiaryRecord()
            # INITIALIZE WS-BENEFICIARY-REC
            beneficiary_rec.BENEF_REC_POLICY = WS_POLICY_NUMBER
            # MOVE WS-POLICY-NUMBER TO BENEF-REC-POLICY
            beneficiary_rec.BENEF_REC_NAME = BENEF_NAME[WS_BENEF_IDX]
            # MOVE BENEF-NAME(WS-BENEF-IDX) TO BENEF-REC-NAME
            beneficiary_rec.BENEF_REC_RELATION = BENEF_RELATION[WS_BENEF_IDX]
            # MOVE BENEF-RELATION(WS-BENEF-IDX) TO BENEF-REC-RELATION
            beneficiary_rec.BENEF_REC_PCT = BENEF_PCT[WS_BENEF_IDX]
            # MOVE BENEF-PCT(WS-BENEF-IDX) TO BENEF-REC-PCT
            # WRITE BENEFICIARY-RECORD FROM WS-BENEFICIARY-REC
            write_beneficiary_record(beneficiary_rec)
        WS_BENEF_IDX += 1

def write_beneficiary_record(beneficiary_rec: BeneficiaryRecord):
    """
    Simulates writing the beneficiary record.
    """
    print(f"Writing beneficiary record: {beneficiary_rec}")

from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal
from datetime import date, datetime


    # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PEP-STATUS
