def handle_pep_match(pep_match_score: Decimal) -> None:
    """Sets PEP status and score."""
    ws_pep_status = 'Y'  # type: str
    ws_pep_score = pep_match_score  # type: Decimal
    return ws_pep_status, ws_pep_score


def check_adverse_media(ws_customer_name: str, media_request: dict) -> int:
    """16116-CHECK-ADVERSE-MEDIA.
    MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO MEDIA-SEARCH-NAME
    CALL 'MEDIASRCH' USING MEDIA-REQUEST MEDIA-RESPONSE
    IF MEDIA-HITS-FOUND > 0
       ADD MEDIA-HITS-FOUND TO WS-WATCHLIST-HITS
    END-IF.
    """
    media_search_name = ws_customer_name  # type: str
    media_response = mediasrch(media_request)  # type: dict # CALL 'MEDIASRCH' USING MEDIA-REQUEST MEDIA-RESPONSE
    media_hits_found = media_response.get("hits", 0) #type: int
    if media_hits_found > 0:
        return media_hits_found
    return 0


def calculate_match_score(ws_ofac_score: Decimal, ws_pep_score: Decimal, ws_watchlist_hits: int) -> Decimal:
    """16120-CALCULATE-MATCH-SCORE.
    IF WS-OFAC-SCORE > 0
       ADD WS-OFAC-SCORE TO WS-MATCH-SCORE
    END-IF
    IF WS-PEP-SCORE > 0
       ADD WS-PEP-SCORE TO WS-MATCH-SCORE
    END-IF
    COMPUTE WS-MATCH-SCORE =
       WS-MATCH-SCORE / WS-WATCHLIST-HITS.
    """
    ws_match_score = Decimal(0)  # type: Decimal
    if ws_ofac_score > Decimal(0):
        ws_match_score += ws_ofac_score
    if ws_pep_score > Decimal(0):
        ws_match_score += ws_pep_score
    if ws_watchlist_hits > 0:
        ws_match_score = ws_match_score / ws_watchlist_hits
    return ws_match_score


def determine_disposition(ws_match_score: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """16130-DETERMINE-DISPOSITION.
    EVALUATE TRUE
       WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 90
          MOVE 'CONFIRMED' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
          MOVE 'Y' TO WS-SAR-REQUIRED
       WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 75
          MOVE 'POTENTIAL' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
          MOVE 'REVIEW' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
       WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 50
          MOVE 'WEAK' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
          MOVE 'CLEARED' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
       WHEN OTHER
          MOVE 'FALSE POSITIVE' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
          MOVE 'CLEARED' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
    END-EVALUATE.
    """
    ws_match_type = ''  # type: str
    ws_sar_required = ''  # type: str
    ws_case_status = ''  # type: str

    if ws_match_score >= Decimal(90):
        ws_match_type = 'CONFIRMED'
        ws_sar_required = 'Y'
    elif ws_match_score >= Decimal(75):
        ws_match_type = 'POTENTIAL'
        ws_case_status = 'REVIEW'
    elif ws_match_score >= Decimal(50):
        ws_match_type = 'WEAK'
        ws_case_status = 'CLEARED'
    else:
        ws_match_type = 'FALSE POSITIVE'
        ws_case_status = 'CLEARED'

    return ws_match_type, ws_sar_required, ws_case_status


def kyc_verification(ws_customer_ssn: str, ws_customer_dob: str, ws_customer_name: str, ws_customer_address: str, ws_doc_type: str, ws_passport_number: str, ws_passport_country: str, ws_license_number: str, ws_license_state: str) -> str:
    """16200-KYC-VERIFICATION.
    PERFORM 16210-VERIFY-IDENTITY
    PERFORM 16220-VERIFY-ADDRESS
    PERFORM 16230-VERIFY-DOCUMENTS
    PERFORM 16240-DETERMINE-KYC-STATUS.
    """
    ws_id_status = verify_identity(ws_customer_ssn, ws_customer_dob, ws_customer_name) # type: str
    ws_addr_status = verify_address(ws_customer_address) # type: str
    ws_doc_status = verify_documents(ws_doc_type, ws_passport_number, ws_passport_country, ws_license_number, ws_license_state) # type: str
    ws_kyc_status = determine_kyc_status(ws_id_status, ws_addr_status, ws_doc_status) # type: str
    return ws_kyc_status


def verify_identity(ws_customer_ssn: str, ws_customer_dob: str, ws_customer_name: str) -> str:
    """16210-VERIFY-IDENTITY.
    MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-SSN TO ID-VERIFY-SSN
    MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-DOB TO ID-VERIFY-DOB
    MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO ID-VERIFY-NAME
    CALL 'IDVERIFY' USING ID-REQUEST ID-RESPONSE
    IF ID-VERIFIED = 'Y'
       MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-ID-STATUS
    ELSE
       MOVE 'FAILED' TO WS-ID-STATUS
    END-IF.
    """
    id_request = {"ssn": ws_customer_ssn, "dob": ws_customer_dob, "name": ws_customer_name}  # type: dict
    id_response = idverify(id_request)  # type: dict # CALL 'IDVERIFY' USING ID-REQUEST ID-RESPONSE
    id_verified = id_response.get("verified", "N") # type: str
    if id_verified == 'Y':
        ws_id_status = 'VERIFIED'  # type: str
    else:
        ws_id_status = 'FAILED'  # type: str
    return ws_id_status


def verify_address(ws_customer_address: str) -> str:
    """16220-VERIFY-ADDRESS.
    MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-ADDRESS TO ADDR-VERIFY-INPUT
    CALL 'ADDRVERIFY' USING ADDR-REQUEST ADDR-RESPONSE
    IF ADDR-VERIFIED = 'Y'
       MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-ADDR-STATUS
    ELSE
       MOVE 'UNVERIFIED' TO WS-ADDR-STATUS
    END-IF.
    """
    addr_request = {"address": ws_customer_address}  # type: dict
    addr_response = addrverify(addr_request)  # type: dict # CALL 'ADDRVERIFY' USING ADDR-REQUEST ADDR-RESPONSE
    addr_verified = addr_response.get("verified", "N") # type: str
    if addr_verified == 'Y':
        ws_addr_status = 'VERIFIED'  # type: str
    else:
        ws_addr_status = 'UNVERIFIED'  # type: str
    return ws_addr_status


def verify_documents(ws_doc_type: str, ws_passport_number: str, ws_passport_country: str, ws_license_number: str, ws_license_state: str) -> str:
    """16230-VERIFY-DOCUMENTS.
    IF WS-DOC-TYPE = 'PASSPORT'
       PERFORM 16232-VERIFY-PASSPORT
    ELSE IF WS-DOC-TYPE = 'LICENSE'
       PERFORM 16234-VERIFY-LICENSE
    ELSE
       PERFORM 16236-VERIFY-OTHER-DOC
    END-IF
    END-IF.
    """
    if ws_doc_type == 'PASSPORT':
        ws_doc_status = verify_passport(ws_passport_number, ws_passport_country) # type: str
    elif ws_doc_type == 'LICENSE':
        ws_doc_status = verify_license(ws_license_number, ws_license_state) # type: str
    else:
        ws_doc_status = verify_other_doc() # type: str
    return ws_doc_status


def verify_passport(ws_passport_number: str, ws_passport_country: str) -> str:
    """16232-VERIFY-PASSPORT.
    MOVE WS-PASSPORT-NUMBER TO PASSPORT-VERIFY-NUM
    MOVE WS-PASSPORT-COUNTRY TO PASSPORT-VERIFY-COUNTRY
    CALL 'PASSVERIFY' USING PASSPORT-REQ PASSPORT-RESP
    IF PASSPORT-VALID = 'Y'
       MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-DOC-STATUS
    ELSE
       MOVE 'INVALID' TO WS-DOC-STATUS
    END-IF.
    """
    passport_req = {"number": ws_passport_number, "country": ws_passport_country}  # type: dict
    passport_resp = passverify(passport_req)  # type: dict # CALL 'PASSVERIFY' USING PASSPORT-REQ PASSPORT-RESP
    passport_valid = passport_resp.get("valid", "N") # type: str
    if passport_valid == 'Y':
        ws_doc_status = 'VERIFIED'  # type: str
    else:
        ws_doc_status = 'INVALID'  # type: str
    return ws_doc_status


def verify_license(ws_license_number: str, ws_license_state: str) -> str:
    """16234-VERIFY-LICENSE.
    MOVE WS-LICENSE-NUMBER TO LICENSE-VERIFY-NUM
    MOVE WS-LICENSE-STATE TO LICENSE-VERIFY-STATE
    CALL 'LICVERIFY' USING LICENSE-REQ LICENSE-RESP
    IF LICENSE-VALID = 'Y'
       MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-DOC-STATUS
    ELSE
       MOVE 'INVALID' TO WS-DOC-STATUS
    END-IF.
    """
    license_req = {"number": ws_license_number, "state": ws_license_state}  # type: dict
    license_resp = licverify(license_req)  # type: dict # CALL 'LICVERIFY' USING LICENSE-REQ LICENSE-RESP
    license_valid = license_resp.get("valid", "N") # type: str
    if license_valid == 'Y':
        ws_doc_status = 'VERIFIED'  # type: str
    else:
        ws_doc_status = 'INVALID'  # type: str
    return ws_doc_status


def verify_other_doc() -> str:
    """16236-VERIFY-OTHER-DOC.
    MOVE 'MANUAL REVIEW' TO WS-DOC-STATUS.
    """
    ws_doc_status = 'MANUAL REVIEW'  # type: str
    return ws_doc_status


def determine_kyc_status(ws_id_status: str, ws_addr_status: str, ws_doc_status: str) -> str:
    """16240-DETERMINE-KYC-STATUS.
    IF WS-ID-STATUS = 'VERIFIED' AND
       WS-ADDR-STATUS = 'VERIFIED' AND
       WS-DOC-STATUS = 'VERIFIED'
       MOVE 'APPROVED' TO WS-KYC-STATUS
    ELSE
       MOVE 'PENDING' TO WS-KYC-STATUS
    END-IF.
    """
    if ws_id_status == 'VERIFIED' and ws_addr_status == 'VERIFIED' and ws_doc_status == 'VERIFIED':
        ws_kyc_status = 'APPROVED'  # type: str
    else:
        ws_kyc_status = 'PENDING'  # type: str
    return ws_kyc_status


def sanctions_check(ws_sanctions_hit: str, ws_customer_id: str, ws_account_status: str, account_record: dict) -> tuple[dict, str, dict]:
    """16300-SANCTIONS-CHECK.
    IF WS-SANCTIONS-HIT = 'Y'
       PERFORM 16310-ESCALATE-TO-COMPLIANCE
       PERFORM 16320-FREEZE-ACCOUNT
    END-IF.
    """
    escalation_record = {}
    freeze_reason = ''
    updated_account_record = account_record
    if ws_sanctions_hit == 'Y':
        escalation_record = escalate_to_compliance(ws_customer_id)
        ws_account_status, freeze_reason, updated_account_record = freeze_account(ws_account_status, account_record)
    return escalation_record, ws_account_status, freeze_reason, updated_account_record


def escalate_to_compliance(ws_customer_id: str) -> dict:
    """16310-ESCALATE-TO-COMPLIANCE.
    INITIALIZE WS-ESCALATION-RECORD
    MOVE 'SANCTIONS HIT' TO ESC-REASON
    MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-ID TO ESC-CUSTOMER
    MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO ESC-DATE
    MOVE 'URGENT' TO ESC-PRIORITY
    WRITE ESCALATION-RECORD FROM WS-ESCALATION-RECORD.
    """
    ws_escalation_record = {}  # type: dict
    esc_reason = 'SANCTIONS HIT'  # type: str
    esc_customer = ws_customer_id  # type: str
    esc_date = date.today()  # type: date
    esc_priority = 'URGENT'  # type: str
    ws_escalation_record["reason"] = esc_reason
    ws_escalation_record["customer"] = esc_customer
    ws_escalation_record["date"] = esc_date
    ws_escalation_record["priority"] = esc_priority
    write_escalation_record(ws_escalation_record) # WRITE ESCALATION-RECORD FROM WS-ESCALATION-RECORD
    return ws_escalation_record


def freeze_account(ws_account_status: str, account_record: dict) -> tuple[str, str, dict]:
    """16320-FREEZE-ACCOUNT.
    MOVE 'F' TO WS-ACCOUNT-STATUS
    MOVE 'SANCTIONS FREEZE' TO WS-FREEZE-REASON
    REWRITE ACCOUNT-RECORD.
    """
    ws_account_status = 'F'  # type: str
    ws_freeze_reason = 'SANCTIONS FREEZE'  # type: str
    account_record["status"] = ws_account_status
    account_record["freeze_reason"] = ws_freeze_reason
    rewrite_account_record(account_record)  # REWRITE ACCOUNT-RECORD
    return ws_account_status, ws_freeze_reason, account_record


def transaction_monitoring(ws_daily_trans_count: int, ws_velocity_threshold: int, ws_daily_trans_amount: Decimal, ws_amount_threshold: Decimal, ws_round_amount_count: int, ws_structuring_detected: str, ws_high_risk_country: str, ws_new_device: str) -> tuple[str, str, Decimal, str]:
    """16400-TRANSACTION-MONITORING.
    PERFORM 16410-CHECK-VELOCITY
    PERFORM 16420-CHECK-PATTERNS
    PERFORM 16430-CHECK-HIGH-RISK
    PERFORM 16440-CALCULATE-RISK-SCORE.
    """
    ws_velocity_flag, ws_amount_flag, ws_fraud_score = check_velocity(ws_daily_trans_count, ws_velocity_threshold, ws_daily_trans_amount, ws_amount_threshold) # type: str, str, Decimal
    ws_pattern_flag, ws_fraud_score = check_patterns(ws_round_amount_count, ws_structuring_detected, ws_fraud_score) # type: str, Decimal
    ws_location_flag, ws_device_flag, ws_fraud_score = check_high_risk(ws_high_risk_country, ws_new_device, ws_fraud_score) # type: str, str, Decimal
    ws_fraud_decision = calculate_risk_score(ws_fraud_score) # type: str
    ws_manual_review = 'N' # type: str

    if ws_fraud_decision in ('BLOCK', 'REVIEW'):
        ws_manual_review = 'Y'
    return ws_velocity_flag, ws_amount_flag, ws_pattern_flag, ws_location_flag, ws_device_flag, ws_fraud_score, ws_fraud_decision, ws_manual_review


def check_velocity(ws_daily_trans_count: int, ws_velocity_threshold: int, ws_daily_trans_amount: Decimal, ws_amount_threshold: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str, Decimal]:
    """16410-CHECK-VELOCITY.
    IF WS-DAILY-TRANS-COUNT > WS-VELOCITY-THRESHOLD
       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-VELOCITY-FLAG
       ADD 20 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
    END-IF
    IF WS-DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT > WS-AMOUNT-THRESHOLD
       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-AMOUNT-FLAG
       ADD 20 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
    END-IF.
    """
    ws_velocity_flag = 'N'  # type: str
    ws_amount_flag = 'N'  # type: str
    ws_fraud_score = Decimal(0)  # type: Decimal

    if ws_daily_trans_count > ws_velocity_threshold:
        ws_velocity_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += Decimal(20)
    if ws_daily_trans_amount > ws_amount_threshold:
        ws_amount_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += Decimal(20)
    return ws_velocity_flag, ws_amount_flag, ws_fraud_score


def check_patterns(ws_round_amount_count: int, ws_structuring_detected: str, ws_fraud_score: Decimal) -> tuple[str, Decimal]:
    """16420-CHECK-PATTERNS.
    IF WS-ROUND-AMOUNT-COUNT > 5
       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PATTERN-FLAG
       ADD 15 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
    END-IF
    IF WS-STRUCTURING-DETECTED = 'Y'
       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PATTERN-FLAG
       ADD 30 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
    END-IF.
    """
    ws_pattern_flag = 'N'  # type: str
    if ws_round_amount_count > 5:
        ws_pattern_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += Decimal(15)
    if ws_structuring_detected == 'Y':
        ws_pattern_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += Decimal(30)
    return ws_pattern_flag, ws_fraud_score


def check_high_risk(ws_high_risk_country: str, ws_new_device: str, ws_fraud_score: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str, Decimal]:
    """16430-CHECK-HIGH-RISK.
    IF WS-HIGH-RISK-COUNTRY = 'Y'
       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-LOCATION-FLAG
       ADD 25 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
    END-IF
    IF WS-NEW-DEVICE = 'Y'
       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-DEVICE-FLAG
       ADD 10 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
    END-IF.
    """
    ws_location_flag = 'N'  # type: str
    ws_device_flag = 'N'  # type: str
    if ws_high_risk_country == 'Y':
        ws_location_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += Decimal(25)
    if ws_new_device == 'Y':
        ws_device_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += Decimal(10)
    return ws_location_flag, ws_device_flag, ws_fraud_score


def calculate_risk_score(ws_fraud_score: Decimal) -> str:
    """16440-CALCULATE-RISK-SCORE.
    EVALUATE TRUE
       WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 80
          MOVE 'BLOCK' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
          MOVE 'Y' TO WS-MANUAL-REVIEW
       WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 60
          MOVE 'REVIEW' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
          MOVE 'Y' TO WS-MANUAL-REVIEW
       WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 40
          MOVE 'MONITOR' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
       WHEN OTHER
          MOVE 'APPROVE' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
    END-EVALUATE.
    """
    match ws_fraud_score:
        case score if score >= Decimal(80):
            ws_fraud_decision = 'BLOCK'  # type: str
        case score if score >= Decimal(60):
            ws_fraud_decision = 'REVIEW'  # type: str
        case score if score >= Decimal(40):
            ws_fraud_decision = 'MONITOR'  # type: str
        case _:
            ws_fraud_decision = 'APPROVE'  # type: str
    return ws_fraud_decision


def suspicious_activity_report(ws_sar_required: str, ws_customer_name: str, ws_customer_address: str, ws_customer_ssn: str, ws_transaction_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """16500-SUSPICIOUS-ACTIVITY-REPORT.
    IF WS-SAR-REQUIRED = 'Y'
       PERFORM 16510-GATHER-SAR-DATA
       PERFORM 16520-GENERATE-SAR
       PERFORM 16530-FILE-SAR
    END-IF.
    """
    if ws_sar_required == 'Y':
        sar_data = gather_sar_data(ws_customer_name, ws_customer_address, ws_customer_ssn, ws_transaction_amount)
        ws_sar_record = generate_sar(sar_data)
        file_sar(ws_sar_record)


def gather_sar_data(ws_customer_name: str, ws_customer_address: str, ws_customer_ssn: str, ws_transaction_amount: Decimal) -> dict:
    """16510-GATHER-SAR-DATA.
    MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO SAR-SUBJECT-NAME
    MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-ADDRESS TO SAR-SUBJECT-ADDR
    MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-SSN TO SAR-SUBJECT-SSN
    MOVE WS-TRANSACTION-AMOUNT TO SAR-AMOUNT
    MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO SAR-ACTIVITY-DATE.
    """
    sar_subject_name = ws_customer_name  # type: str
    sar_subject_addr = ws_customer_address  # type: str
    sar_subject_ssn = ws_customer_ssn  # type: str
    sar_amount = ws_transaction_amount  # type: Decimal
    sar_activity_date = date.today()  # type: date
    return {
        "subject_name": sar_subject_name,
        "subject_addr": sar_subject_addr,
        "subject_ssn": sar_subject_ssn,
        "amount": sar_amount,
        "activity_date": sar_activity_date
    }

def generate_sar(sar_data: dict) -> dict:
    """16520-GENERATE-SAR.
    INITIALIZE WS-SAR-RECORD
    MOVE SAR-SUBJECT-NAME TO SAR-REC-NAME
    MOVE SAR-SUBJECT-ADDR TO SAR-REC-ADDR
    MOVE SAR-AMOUNT TO SAR-REC-AMOUNT
    MOVE SAR-ACTIVITY-DATE TO SAR-REC-DATE
    MOVE 'SUSPICIOUS PATTERN DETECTED' TO SAR-REC-NARRATIVE.
    """
    ws_sar_record = {}  # type: dict
    sar_rec_name = sar_data["subject_name"]  # type: str
    sar_rec_addr = sar_data["subject_addr"]  # type: str
    sar_rec_amount = sar_data["amount"]  # type: Decimal
    sar_rec_date = sar_data["activity_date"]  # type: date
    sar_rec_narrative = 'SUSPICIOUS PATTERN DETECTED'  # type: str

    ws_sar_record["name"] = sar_rec_name
    ws_sar_record["address"] = sar_rec_addr
    ws_sar_record["amount"] = sar_rec_amount
    ws_sar_record["date"] = sar_rec_date
    ws_sar_record["narrative"] = sar_rec_narrative
    return ws_sar_record


def file_sar(ws_sar_record: dict) -> None:
    """16530-FILE-SAR.
    MOVE 'PENDING' TO SAR-STATUS
    WRITE SAR-RECORD FROM WS-SAR-RECORD.
    """
    sar_status = 'PENDING'  # type: str
    ws_sar_record["status"] = sar_status
    write_sar_record(ws_sar_record)  # WRITE SAR-RECORD FROM WS-SAR-RECORD


def customer_service(ws_open_date: date, ws_case_type: str, ws_customer_account: str, ws_customer_id: str, ws_channel: str, ws_billing_error: str, ws_credit_amount: Decimal, ws_follow_up_required: str, ws_customer_phone: str) -> None:
    """17000-CUSTOMER-SERVICE.
    PERFORM 17100-CREATE-CASE
    PERFORM 17200-ROUTE-CASE
    PERFORM 17300-PROCESS-CASE
    PERFORM 17400-RESOLVE-CASE
    PERFORM 17500-FOLLOW-UP.
    """
    ws_case_id, ws_case_priority, ws_target_date, ws_case_status = create_case(ws_open_date, ws_case_type) # type: str, int, date
    ws_queue, ws_assigned_agent, ws_case_status = route_case(ws_case_type) # type: str, str, str
    ws_interaction_count, ws_research_notes, ws_previous_case_count, ws_caller_type, ws_resolution_code, ws_fraud_case, ws_reset_request, ws_card_request = process_case(ws_customer_account, ws_customer_id, ws_channel, ws_billing_error, ws_credit_amount) # type: int, str, int, str, str, str, dict, dict
    ws_case_status, ws_close_date, ws_case_update = resolve_case(ws_case_id, ws_case_status, ws_resolution_code) # type: str, date, dict
    follow_up(ws_case_id, ws_follow_up_required, ws_close_date, ws_customer_phone) # type: str, str, date, str


def create_case(ws_open_date: date, ws_case_type: str) -> tuple[str, int, date, str]:
    """17100-CREATE-CASE.
    PERFORM 17110-GENERATE-CASE-ID
    MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-OPEN-DATE
    MOVE 'OPEN' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
    PERFORM 17120-CATEGORIZE-CASE.
    """
    ws_case_id = generate_case_id() # type: str
    ws_case_status = 'OPEN'  # type: str
    ws_case_priority, ws_target_date = categorize_case(ws_open_date, ws_case_type) # type: int, date
    return ws_case_id, ws_case_priority, ws_target_date, ws_case_status


def generate_case_id() -> str:
    """17110-GENERATE-CASE-ID.
    MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-DATE-PART
    COMPUTE WS-RANDOM-PART = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999
    STRING 'CS' DELIMITED SIZE
           WS-DATE-PART DELIMITED SIZE
           WS-RANDOM-PART DELIMITED SIZE
       INTO WS-CASE-ID.
    """
    ws_date_part = date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d") # type: str
    ws_random_part = str(int(random_number() * 99999)).zfill(5) # type: str
    ws_case_id = f'CS{ws_date_part}{ws_random_part}'  # type: str
    return ws_case_id


def categorize_case(ws_open_date: date, ws_case_type: str) -> tuple[int, date]:
    """17120-CATEGORIZE-CASE.
    EVALUATE WS-CASE-TYPE
       WHEN 'BILLING INQUIRY'
          MOVE 2 TO WS-CASE-PRIORITY
       WHEN 'FRAUD REPORT'
          MOVE 1 TO WS-CASE-PRIORITY
       WHEN 'ACCOUNT ACCESS'
          MOVE 1 TO WS-CASE-PRIORITY
       WHEN 'GENERAL INQUIRY'
          MOVE 3 TO WS-CASE-PRIORITY
       WHEN OTHER
          MOVE 3 TO WS-CASE-PRIORITY
    END-EVALUATE
    COMPUTE WS-TARGET-DATE =
       FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-OPEN-DATE) +
       WS-CASE-PRIORITY * 2.
    """
    match ws_case_type:
        case 'BILLING INQUIRY':
            ws_case_priority = 2  # type: int
        case 'FRAUD REPORT':
            ws_case_priority = 1  # type: int
        case 'ACCOUNT ACCESS':
            ws_case_priority = 1  # type: int
        case 'GENERAL INQUIRY':
            ws_case_priority = 3  # type: int
        case _:
            ws_case_priority = 3  # type: int
    ws_target_date = ws_open_date + timedelta(days=ws_case_priority * 2)  # type: date
    return ws_case_priority, ws_target_date


def route_case(ws_case_type: str) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
    """17200-ROUTE-CASE.
    EVALUATE WS-CASE-TYPE
       WHEN 'BILLING INQUIRY'
          MOVE 'BILLING' TO WS-QUEUE
       WHEN 'FRAUD REPORT'
          MOVE 'FRAUD' TO WS-QUEUE
       WHEN 'ACCOUNT ACCESS'
          MOVE 'SECURITY' TO WS-QUEUE
       WHEN 'LOAN INQUIRY'
          MOVE 'LENDING' TO WS-QUEUE
       WHEN OTHER
          MOVE 'GENERAL' TO WS-QUEUE
    END-EVALUATE
    PERFORM 17210-ASSIGN-AGENT.
    """
    match ws_case_type:
        case 'BILLING INQUIRY':
            ws_queue = 'BILLING'  # type: str
        case 'FRAUD REPORT':
            ws_queue = 'FRAUD'  # type: str
        case 'ACCOUNT ACCESS':
            ws_queue = 'SECURITY'  # type: str
        case 'LOAN INQUIRY':
            ws_queue = 'LENDING'  # type: str
        case _:
            ws_queue = 'GENERAL'  # type: str

    ws_assigned_agent, ws_case_status = assign_agent(ws_queue)
    return ws_queue, ws_assigned_agent, ws_case_status


def assign_agent(ws_queue: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """17210-ASSIGN-AGENT.
    CALL 'ROUTECASE' USING WS-QUEUE WS-ASSIGNED-AGENT
    IF WS-ASSIGNED-AGENT = SPACES
       MOVE 'UNASSIGNED' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
    ELSE
       MOVE 'ASSIGNED' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
    END-IF.
    """
    ws_assigned_agent = routecase(ws_queue)  # type: str # CALL 'ROUTECASE' USING WS-QUEUE WS-ASSIGNED-AGENT
    if not ws_assigned_agent: # IF WS-ASSIGNED-AGENT = SPACES
        ws_case_status = 'UNASSIGNED'  # type: str
    else:
        ws_case_status = 'ASSIGNED'  # type: str
    return ws_assigned_agent, ws_case_status


def process_case(ws_customer_account: str, ws_customer_id: str, ws_channel: str, ws_billing_error: str, ws_credit_amount: Decimal) -> tuple[int, str, int, str, str, str, dict, dict]:
    """17300-PROCESS-CASE.
    PERFORM 17310-LOG-INTERACTION
    PERFORM 17320-RESEARCH-ISSUE
    PERFORM 17330-DETERMINE-RESOLUTION.
    """
    ws_interaction_count = log_interaction(ws_channel) # type: int
    ws_research_notes, ws_previous_case_count, ws_caller_type = research_issue(ws_customer_account, ws_customer_id) # type: str, int, str
    ws_resolution_code, ws_fraud_

    def update_next_run_date(self, frequency: str):
        """
        000015               FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 1
        000016         WHEN 'WEEKLY'
        000017            COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE =
        000018               FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 7
        000019         WHEN 'MONTHLY'
        000020            COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE =
        000021               FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 30
        000022         WHEN 'QUARTERLY'
        000023            COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE =
        000024               FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 90
        000025         WHEN 'YEARLY'
        000026            COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE =
        000027               FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 365
        000028      END-EVALUATE.
        """
        last_run_date_int = self.integer_of_date(self.WS_LAST_RUN_DATE)
        match frequency:
            case 'WEEKLY':
                self.WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = last_run_date_int + 7
            case 'MONTHLY':
                self.WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = last_run_date_int + 30
            case 'QUARTERLY':
                self.WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = last_run_date_int + 90
            case 'YEARLY':
                self.WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = last_run_date_int + 365
            case _:
                self.WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = last_run_date_int + 1

    def integer_of_date(self, date: str) -> int:
        """Dummy function for INTEGER-OF-DATE"""
        return int(date)

    def random(self) -> float:
        """Dummy function for RANDOM"""
        return 0.5

    def mod(self, a: int, b: int) -> int:
        """Dummy function for MOD"""
        return a % b

    def string(self, *args, delimiter: str = " ") -> str:
        """Dummy function for STRING"""
        result = ""
        for arg in args:
            result += str(arg) + delimiter
        return result.rstrip(delimiter)

    def collect_metrics(self):
        """
        000039    21100-COLLECT-METRICS.
        """
        self.collect_transaction_metrics()  # 000040    PERFORM 21110-COLLECT-TRANSACTION-METRICS
        self.collect_customer_metrics()  # 000041    PERFORM 21120-COLLECT-CUSTOMER-METRICS
        self.collect_performance_metrics()  # 000042    PERFORM 21130-COLLECT-PERFORMANCE-METRICS.

    def collect_transaction_metrics(self):
        """
        000044    21110-COLLECT-TRANSACTION-METRICS.
        """
        self.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal(0)  # 000045    MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT
        self.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT = 0  # 000046    MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT
        self.WS_AVG_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal(0)  # 000047    MOVE ZEROES TO WS-AVG-TRANS-AMOUNT
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # 000048    PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
            if self.TRANSACTION_FILE:
                self.WS_TRANS_REC = self.TRANSACTION_FILE.pop(0)
                self.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT += 1  # 000053       ADD 1 TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT
                self.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT += self.WS_TRANS_REC.trans_amount  # 000054       ADD TRANS-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT
            else:
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # 000051       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
        if self.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT > 0:  # 000057    IF WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT > 0
            self.WS_AVG_TRANS_AMOUNT = self.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT / self.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT  # 000058       COMPUTE WS-AVG-TRANS-AMOUNT =
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # 000061    MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.

    def collect_customer_metrics(self):
        """
        000063    21120-COLLECT-CUSTOMER-METRICS.
        """
        self.WS_ACTIVE_CUSTOMERS = 0  # 000064    MOVE ZEROES TO WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS
        self.WS_NEW_CUSTOMERS = 0  # 000065    MOVE ZEROES TO WS-NEW-CUSTOMERS
        self.WS_CHURNED_CUSTOMERS = 0  # 000066    MOVE ZEROES TO WS-CHURNED-CUSTOMERS
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # 000067    PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
            if self.CUSTOMER_FILE:
                self.WS_CUST_REC = self.CUSTOMER_FILE.pop(0)
                if self.WS_CUST_REC.cust_status == 'A':  # 000072          IF CUST-STATUS = 'A'
                    self.WS_ACTIVE_CUSTOMERS += 1  # 000073             ADD 1 TO WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS
                if self.WS_CUST_REC.cust_open_date >= self.WS_PERIOD_START:  # 000076          IF CUST-OPEN-DATE >= WS-PERIOD-START
                    self.WS_NEW_CUSTOMERS += 1  # 000077             ADD 1 TO WS-NEW-CUSTOMERS
                if self.WS_CUST_REC.cust_close_date >= self.WS_PERIOD_START:  # 000080          IF CUST-CLOSE-DATE >= WS-PERIOD-START
                    self.WS_CHURNED_CUSTOMERS += 1  # 000081             ADD 1 TO WS-CHURNED-CUSTOMERS
            else:
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # 000070       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # 000085    MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.

    def collect_performance_metrics(self):
        """
        000087    21130-COLLECT-PERFORMANCE-METRICS.
        """
        self.WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL = Decimal(0)  # 000088    MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL
        self.WS_RESPONSE_COUNT = 0  # 000089    MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RESPONSE-COUNT
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # 000090    PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
            if self.PERF_LOG_FILE:
                self.WS_PERF_REC = self.PERF_LOG_FILE.pop(0)
                self.WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL += self.WS_PERF_REC.perf_response_time  # 000095       ADD PERF-RESPONSE-TIME TO WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL
                self.WS_RESPONSE_COUNT += 1  # 000096       ADD 1 TO WS-RESPONSE-COUNT
            else:
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # 000093       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
        if self.WS_RESPONSE_COUNT > 0:  # 000099    IF WS-RESPONSE-COUNT > 0
            self.WS_AVG_RESPONSE_TIME = self.WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL / self.WS_RESPONSE_COUNT  # 000100       COMPUTE WS-AVG-RESPONSE-TIME =
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # 000103    MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.

    def aggregate_data(self):
        """
        000105    21200-AGGREGATE-DATA.
        """
        self.daily_aggregation()  # 000106    PERFORM 21210-DAILY-AGGREGATION
        self.weekly_aggregation()  # 000107    PERFORM 21220-WEEKLY-AGGREGATION
        self.monthly_aggregation()  # 000108    PERFORM 21230-MONTHLY-AGGREGATION.

    def daily_aggregation(self):
        """
        000110    21210-DAILY-AGGREGATION.
        """
        self.WS_DAILY_SUMMARY = WsDailySummary()  # 000111    INITIALIZE WS-DAILY-SUMMARY
        self.WS_DAILY_SUMMARY.daily_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE  # 000112    MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO DAILY-DATE
        self.WS_DAILY_SUMMARY.daily_trans_count = self.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT  # 000113    MOVE WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT TO DAILY-TRANS-COUNT
        self.WS_DAILY_SUMMARY.daily_trans_amount = self.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT  # 000114    MOVE WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT TO DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT
        self.WS_DAILY_SUMMARY.daily_deposits = self.WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS  # 000115    MOVE WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS TO DAILY-DEPOSITS
        self.WS_DAILY_SUMMARY.daily_withdrawals = self.WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS  # 000116    MOVE WS-TOTAL-WITHDRAWALS TO DAILY-WITHDRAWALS
        # WRITE DAILY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-DAILY-SUMMARY.  # 000117    WRITE DAILY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-DAILY-SUMMARY.

    def weekly_aggregation(self):
        """
        000119    21220-WEEKLY-AGGREGATION.
        """
        if self.WS_DAY_OF_WEEK == 7:  # 000120    IF WS-DAY-OF-WEEK = 7
            self.WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY = WsWeeklySummary()  # 000121       INITIALIZE WS-WEEKLY-SUMMARY
            self.WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY.weekly_week = self.WS_WEEK_NUMBER  # 000122       MOVE WS-WEEK-NUMBER TO WEEKLY-WEEK
            self.sum_week_data()  # 000123       PERFORM 21225-SUM-WEEK-DATA
            # WRITE WEEKLY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-WEEKLY-SUMMARY  # 000124       WRITE WEEKLY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-WEEKLY-SUMMARY

    def sum_week_data(self):
        """
        000126    21225-SUM-WEEK-DATA.
        """
        self.WEEKLY_TRANS_COUNT = 0  # 000127    MOVE ZEROES TO WEEKLY-TRANS-COUNT
        self.WEEKLY_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal(0)  # 000128    MOVE ZEROES TO WEEKLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
        for _ in range(7):  # 000129    PERFORM 7 TIMES
            self.WEEKLY_TRANS_COUNT += self.DAILY_TRANS_COUNT  # 000130       ADD DAILY-TRANS-COUNT TO WEEKLY-TRANS-COUNT
            self.WEEKLY_TRANS_AMOUNT += self.DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT  # 000131       ADD DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT TO WEEKLY-TRANS-AMOUNT

    def monthly_aggregation(self):
        """
        000135    21230-MONTHLY-AGGREGATION.
        """
        if self.WS_END_OF_MONTH == 'Y':  # 000136    IF WS-END-OF-MONTH = 'Y'
            self.WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY = WsMonthlySummary()  # 000137       INITIALIZE WS-MONTHLY-SUMMARY
            self.WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY.monthly_month = self.WS_CURR_MONTH  # 000138       MOVE WS-CURR-MONTH TO MONTHLY-MONTH
            self.WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY.monthly_year = self.WS_CURR_YEAR  # 000139       MOVE WS-CURR-YEAR TO MONTHLY-YEAR
            self.sum_month_data()  # 000140       PERFORM 21235-SUM-MONTH-DATA
            # WRITE MONTHLY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-MONTHLY-SUMMARY  # 000141       WRITE MONTHLY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-MONTHLY-SUMMARY

    def sum_month_data(self):
        """
        000143    21235-SUM-MONTH-DATA.
        """
        self.MONTHLY_TRANS_COUNT = 0  # 000144    MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-TRANS-COUNT
        self.MONTHLY_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal(0)  # 000145    MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
        self.MONTHLY_NEW_ACCOUNTS = 0  # 000146    MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-NEW-ACCOUNTS
        self.MONTHLY_CLOSED_ACCOUNTS = 0  # 000147    MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-CLOSED-ACCOUNTS
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # 000148    PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
            if self.DAILY_SUMMARY_FILE:
                self.WS_DAILY_SUM_REC = self.DAILY_SUMMARY_FILE.pop(0)
                if self.WS_DAILY_SUM_REC.daily_month == self.WS_CURR_MONTH:  # 000153          IF DAILY-MONTH = WS-CURR-MONTH
                    self.MONTHLY_TRANS_COUNT += self.WS_DAILY_SUM_REC.daily_trans_count  # 000154             ADD DAILY-TRANS-COUNT TO MONTHLY-TRANS-COUNT
                    self.MONTHLY_TRANS_AMOUNT += self.WS_DAILY_SUM_REC.daily_trans_amount  # 000155             ADD DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT TO MONTHLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
            else:
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # 000151       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # 000158    MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.

    def calculate_kpi(self):
        """
        000160    21300-CALCULATE-KPI.
        """
        self.calc_financial_kpi()  # 000161    PERFORM 21310-CALC-FINANCIAL-KPI
        self.calc_operational_kpi()  # 000162    PERFORM 21320-CALC-OPERATIONAL-KPI
        self.calc_customer_kpi()  # 000163    PERFORM 21330-CALC-CUSTOMER-KPI.

    def calc_financial_kpi(self):
        """
        000165    21310-CALC-FINANCIAL-KPI.
        """
        if self.WS_TOTAL_ASSETS > 0:  # 000166    IF WS-TOTAL-ASSETS > 0
            self.WS_ROA = (self.WS_NET_INCOME / self.WS_TOTAL_ASSETS) * 100  # 000167       COMPUTE WS-ROA =
        if self.WS_TOTAL_EQUITY > 0:  # 000170    IF WS-TOTAL-EQUITY > 0
            self.WS_ROE = (self.WS_NET_INCOME / self.WS_TOTAL_EQUITY) * 100  # 000171       COMPUTE WS-ROE =
        if self.WS_INTEREST_EXPENSE > 0:  # 000

def integer_of_date(date: str) -> int:
    """FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE"""
    return int(date.replace("-", ""))

def validate_wire_request():
    """24100-VALIDATE-WIRE-REQUEST."""
    global WS_WIRE_VALID, WS_WIRE_REJECT, WS_CTR_REQUIRED
    WS_WIRE_VALID = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT <= 0:  # IF WS-WIRE-AMOUNT <= 0
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INVALID AMOUNT'  # MOVE 'INVALID AMOUNT' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:  # IF WS-WIRE-AMOUNT > WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'  # MOVE 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
    if WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT == "":  # IF WS-BENEFICIARY-ACCOUNT = SPACES
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED'  # MOVE 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > 10000:  # IF WS-WIRE-AMOUNT > 10000
        WS_CTR_REQUIRED = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CTR-REQUIRED

def ofac_screening():
    """24200-OFAC-SCREENING."""
    global WS_OFAC_CLEAR, OFAC_SEARCH_NAME, OFAC_SEARCH_BANK, WS_WIRE_REJECT
    WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-OFAC-CLEAR
    OFAC_SEARCH_NAME = WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME  # MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-NAME TO OFAC-SEARCH-NAME
    ofac_request = OFACRequest() #initialize
    ofac_response = OFACResponse()
    ofacsrch(ofac_request, ofac_response)  # CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING OFAC-REQUEST OFAC-RESPONSE
    if ofac_response.ofac_match_found == 'Y':  # IF OFAC-MATCH-FOUND = 'Y'
        if ofac_response.ofac_match_score >= 85:  # IF OFAC-MATCH-SCORE >= 85
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-OFAC-CLEAR
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'OFAC MATCH'  # MOVE 'OFAC MATCH' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
    OFAC_SEARCH_BANK = WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK  # MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-BANK TO OFAC-SEARCH-BANK
    ofac_request = OFACRequest() #reinitialize
    ofac_response = OFACResponse()
    ofacsrch(ofac_request, ofac_response)  # CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING OFAC-REQUEST OFAC-RESPONSE
    if ofac_response.ofac_match_found == 'Y':  # IF OFAC-MATCH-FOUND = 'Y'
        if ofac_response.ofac_match_score >= 85:  # IF OFAC-MATCH-SCORE >= 85
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-OFAC-CLEAR
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BANK OFAC MATCH'  # MOVE 'BANK OFAC MATCH' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT

def process_wire():
    """24300-PROCESS-WIRE."""
    debit_originator()  # PERFORM 24310-DEBIT-ORIGINATOR
    create_wire_message()  # PERFORM 24320-CREATE-WIRE-MESSAGE
    transmit_wire()  # PERFORM 24330-TRANSMIT-WIRE
    record_wire()  # PERFORM 24340-RECORD-WIRE

def debit_originator():
    """24310-DEBIT-ORIGINATOR."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= WS_WIRE_AMOUNT  # SUBTRACT WS-WIRE-AMOUNT FROM WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= WS_WIRE_FEE  # SUBTRACT WS-WIRE-FEE FROM WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
    update_account()  # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT

def create_wire_message():
    """24320-CREATE-WIRE-MESSAGE."""
    global SWIFT_MSG_TYPE, SWIFT_TXN_REF, SWIFT_VALUE_DATE, SWIFT_CURRENCY, SWIFT_AMOUNT, \
           SWIFT_ORDERING_CUST, SWIFT_ORDERING_ACCT, SWIFT_BENEF_CUST, SWIFT_BENEF_ACCT, \
           SWIFT_BENEF_BANK, SWIFT_REMIT_INFO
    # INITIALIZE WS-SWIFT-MESSAGE
    SWIFT_MSG_TYPE = 'MT103'  # MOVE 'MT103' TO SWIFT-MSG-TYPE
    SWIFT_TXN_REF = WS_WIRE_REF  # MOVE WS-WIRE-REF TO SWIFT-TXN-REF
    SWIFT_VALUE_DATE = WS_WIRE_DATE  # MOVE WS-WIRE-DATE TO SWIFT-VALUE-DATE
    SWIFT_CURRENCY = WS_WIRE_CURRENCY  # MOVE WS-WIRE-CURRENCY TO SWIFT-CURRENCY
    SWIFT_AMOUNT = WS_WIRE_AMOUNT  # MOVE WS-WIRE-AMOUNT TO SWIFT-AMOUNT
    SWIFT_ORDERING_CUST = WS_ORIGINATOR_NAME  # MOVE WS-ORIGINATOR-NAME TO SWIFT-ORDERING-CUST
    SWIFT_ORDERING_ACCT = WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT  # MOVE WS-ORIGINATOR-ACCOUNT TO SWIFT-ORDERING-ACCT
    SWIFT_BENEF_CUST = WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME  # MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-NAME TO SWIFT-BENEF-CUST
    SWIFT_BENEF_ACCT = WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT  # MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-ACCOUNT TO SWIFT-BENEF-ACCT
    SWIFT_BENEF_BANK = WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK_BIC  # MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-BANK-BIC TO SWIFT-BENEF-BANK
    SWIFT_REMIT_INFO = WS_PURPOSE  # MOVE WS-PURPOSE TO SWIFT-REMIT-INFO

def transmit_wire():
    """24330-TRANSMIT-WIRE."""
    global WS_WIRE_STATUS
    swift_message = WSSwiftMessage() #initialize
    swift_response = WSSwiftResponse()
    swiftsend(swift_message, swift_response)  # CALL 'SWIFTSEND' USING WS-SWIFT-MESSAGE WS-SWIFT-RESPONSE
    if swift_response.swift_status == 'ACK':  # IF SWIFT-STATUS = 'ACK'
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'SENT'  # MOVE 'SENT' TO WS-WIRE-STATUS
    else:  # ELSE
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'FAILED'  # MOVE 'FAILED' TO WS-WIRE-STATUS
        reverse_debit()  # PERFORM 24350-REVERSE-DEBIT

def record_wire():
    """24340-RECORD-WIRE."""
    global WIRE_REF, WIRE_AMOUNT, WIRE_STATUS, WIRE_FROM_ACCT, WIRE_TO_ACCT, WIRE_DATE
    # INITIALIZE WS-WIRE-RECORD
    WIRE_REF = WS_WIRE_REF  # MOVE WS-WIRE-REF TO WIRE-REF
    WIRE_AMOUNT = WS_WIRE_AMOUNT  # MOVE WS-WIRE-AMOUNT TO WIRE-AMOUNT
    WIRE_STATUS = WS_WIRE_STATUS  # MOVE WS-WIRE-STATUS TO WIRE-STATUS
    WIRE_FROM_ACCT = WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT  # MOVE WS-ORIGINATOR-ACCOUNT TO WIRE-FROM-ACCT
    WIRE_TO_ACCT = WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT  # MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-ACCOUNT TO WIRE-TO-ACCT
    WIRE_DATE = WS_PROCESS_DATE  # MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO WIRE-DATE
    wire_record = WSWireRecord()
    write_wire_record(wire_record)  # WRITE WIRE-RECORD FROM WS-WIRE-RECORD

def reverse_debit():
    """24350-REVERSE-DEBIT."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += WS_WIRE_AMOUNT  # ADD WS-WIRE-AMOUNT TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += WS_WIRE_FEE  # ADD WS-WIRE-FEE TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
    update_account()  # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT

def send_confirmation():
    """24400-SEND-CONFIRMATION."""
    global WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'WIRE-CONFIRM'  # MOVE 'WIRE-CONFIRM' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'  # MOVE 'EMAIL' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'Wire transfer {WS_WIRE_REF} completed' # STRING 'Wire transfer ' DELIMITED SIZE WS-WIRE-REF DELIMITED SIZE ' completed' DELIMITED SIZE INTO WS-NOTIF-SUBJECT
    send_notification()  # PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION

def reject_wire():
    """24500-REJECT-WIRE."""
    global WS_WIRE_STATUS, REJECT_WIRE_REF, REJECT_REASON, REJECT_DATE, WS_NOTIF_TYPE
    WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'REJECTED'  # MOVE 'REJECTED' TO WS-WIRE-STATUS
    # INITIALIZE WS-WIRE-REJECT-REC
    REJECT_WIRE_REF = WS_WIRE_REF  # MOVE WS-WIRE-REF TO REJECT-WIRE-REF
    REJECT_REASON = WS_WIRE_REJECT  # MOVE WS-WIRE-REJECT TO REJECT-REASON
    REJECT_DATE = WS_PROCESS_DATE  # MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO REJECT-DATE
    wire_reject_rec = WSWireRejectRec()
    write_wire_reject_record(wire_reject_rec)  # WRITE WIRE-REJECT-RECORD FROM WS-WIRE-REJECT-REC
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'WIRE-REJECTED'  # MOVE 'WIRE-REJECTED' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
    send_notification()  # PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION

def ach_processing():
    """25000-ACH-PROCESSING."""
    receive_ach_file()  # PERFORM 25100-RECEIVE-ACH-FILE
    validate_ach_entries()  # PERFORM 25200-VALIDATE-ACH-ENTRIES
    process_ach_credits()  # PERFORM 25300-PROCESS-ACH-CREDITS
    process_ach_debits()  # PERFORM 25400-PROCESS-ACH-DEBITS
    generate_ach_return()  # PERFORM 25500-GENERATE-ACH-RETURN

def receive_ach_file():
    """25100-RECEIVE-ACH-FILE."""
    global WS_CURRENT_ACH_FILE, WS_ACH_FILE_DATE, WS_EXPECTED_ENTRIES
    # OPEN INPUT ACH-INPUT-FILE
    ach_file_header = WSAchFileHeader() #simulate reading
    WS_CURRENT_ACH_FILE = ach_file_header.ach_file_id  # MOVE ACH-FILE-ID TO WS-CURRENT-ACH-FILE
    WS_ACH_FILE_DATE = ach_file_header.ach_creation_date  # MOVE ACH-CREATION-DATE TO WS-ACH-FILE-DATE
    WS_EXPECTED_ENTRIES = ach_file_header.ach_entry_count  # MOVE ACH-ENTRY-COUNT TO WS-EXPECTED-ENTRIES

def validate_ach_entries():
    """25200-VALIDATE-ACH-ENTRIES."""
    global WS_VALID_ENTRIES, WS_INVALID_ENTRIES, WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_VALID_ENTRIES = 0  # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-VALID-ENTRIES
    WS_INVALID_ENTRIES = 0  # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-INVALID-ENTRIES
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y': # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
        ach_entry = read_ach_input_file() #simulate read
        if ach_entry is None: #end of file
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            validate_single_entry(ach_entry)

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

def validate_single_entry(ach_entry: WSAchEntry):
    """25210-VALIDATE-SINGLE-ENTRY."""
    global WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE, WS_VALID_ENTRIES, WS_INVALID_ENTRIES
    WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-ACH-ENTRY-VALID
    if not ach_entry.ach_routing.isnumeric():  # IF ACH-ROUTING NOT NUMERIC
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-ACH-ENTRY-VALID
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R03'  # MOVE 'R03' TO WS-ACH-RETURN-CODE
    if ach_entry.ach_account == "":  # IF ACH-ACCOUNT = SPACES
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-ACH-ENTRY-VALID
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'  # MOVE 'R04' TO WS-ACH-RETURN-CODE
    if ach_entry.ach_amount <= 0:  # IF ACH-AMOUNT <= 0
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-ACH-ENTRY-VALID
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R06'  # MOVE 'R06' TO WS-ACH-RETURN-CODE
    if WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID == 'Y':  # IF WS-ACH-ENTRY-VALID = 'Y'
        WS_VALID_ENTRIES += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-VALID-ENTRIES
    else:  # ELSE
        WS_INVALID_ENTRIES += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-INVALID-ENTRIES

def process_ach_credits():
    """25300-PROCESS-ACH-CREDITS."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y': # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
        ach_entry = read_ach_input_file() #simulate read
        if ach_entry is None: #end of file
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            if ach_entry.ach_trans_code in ('22', '23', '32', '33'): # IF ACH-TRANS-CODE = '22' OR '23' OR '32' OR '33'
                apply_credit(ach_entry) # PERFORM 25310-APPLY-CREDIT
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

def apply_credit(ach_entry: WSAchEntry):
    """25310-APPLY-CREDIT."""
    global WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_FLAG, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_CREDITS_POSTED, WS_TOTAL_CREDITS, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE
    WS_SEARCH_KEY = ach_entry.ach_account  # MOVE ACH-ACCOUNT TO WS-SEARCH-KEY
    search_account()  # PERFORM 5000-SEARCH-ACCOUNT
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':  # IF WS-FOUND-FLAG = 'Y'
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += ach_entry.ach_amount  # ADD ACH-AMOUNT TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        update_account()  # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
        WS_CREDITS_POSTED += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-CREDITS-POSTED
        WS_TOTAL_CREDITS += ach_entry.ach_amount  # ADD ACH-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-CREDITS
    else:  # ELSE
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'  # MOVE 'R04' TO WS-ACH-RETURN-CODE
        create_return_entry(ach_entry)  # PERFORM 25510-CREATE-RETURN-ENTRY

def process_ach_debits():
    """25400-PROCESS-ACH-DEBITS."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y': # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
        ach_entry = read_ach_input_file() #simulate read
        if ach_entry is None: #end of file
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            if ach_entry.ach_trans_code in ('27', '28', '37', '38'): # IF ACH-TRANS-CODE = '27' OR '28' OR '37' OR '38'
                apply_debit(ach_entry) # PERFORM 25410-APPLY-DEBIT
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

def apply_debit(ach_entry: WSAchEntry):
    """25410-APPLY-DEBIT."""
    global WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_FLAG, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_DEBITS_POSTED, WS_TOTAL_DEBITS, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE
    WS_SEARCH_KEY = ach_entry.ach_account  # MOVE ACH-ACCOUNT TO WS-SEARCH-KEY
    search_account()  # PERFORM 5000-SEARCH-ACCOUNT
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':  # IF WS-FOUND-FLAG = 'Y'
        if WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE >= ach_entry.ach_amount:  # IF WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE >= ACH-AMOUNT
            WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= ach_entry.ach_amount  # SUBTRACT ACH-AMOUNT FROM WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
            update_account()  # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
            WS_DEBITS_POSTED += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-DEBITS-POSTED
            WS_TOTAL_DEBITS += ach_entry.ach_amount  # ADD ACH-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-DEBITS
        else:  # ELSE
            WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R01'  # MOVE 'R01' TO WS-ACH-RETURN-CODE
            create_return_entry(ach_entry)  # PERFORM 2551

    def validate_stop_request(self):
        """29100-VALIDATE-STOP-REQUEST."""
        self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'Y' # 29101
        if self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER == "0" * len(self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER): # Assuming zeroes means all zeros
            self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'N' # 29103
            self.WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK NUMBER REQUIRED' # 29104
        if self.WS_CHECK_ALREADY_CLEARED == 'Y': # 29106
            self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'N' # 29107
            self.WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED' # 29108

    def create_stop_order(self):
        """29200-CREATE-STOP-ORDER."""
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD = WsStopRecord() # 29201 - Initialize WS_STOP_RECORD
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_account = self.ACCT_ID # 29202
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_check_number = self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER # 29203
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_amount = self.WS_CHECK_AMOUNT # 29204
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_payee = self.WS_PAYEE_NAME # 29205
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_effective_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE # 29206
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_expiry_date = self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 180 # 29207-29208
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_status = 'A' # 29209
        self.write_stop_record() # 29210

    def apply_stop_fee(self):
        """29300-APPLY-STOP-FEE."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_STOP_PAYMENT_FEE # 29301
        self.update_account() # 29302
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'STOP-PAYMENT' # 29303
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL' # 29304
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f"Stop payment placed on check # {self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER}"  # 29305-29307
        self.send_notification() # 29308

    def safe_deposit_box(self):
        """30000-SAFE-DEPOSIT-BOX."""
        self.box_rental() # 30001
        self.box_access() # 30002
        self.box_drilling() # 30003
        self.box_billing() # 30004

    def box_rental(self):
        """30100-BOX-RENTAL."""
        if self.WS_RENTAL_REQUEST == 'Y': # 30101
            self.check_availability() # 30102
            if self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE == 'Y': # 30103
                self.assign_box() # 30104
                self.create_rental_agreement() # 30105

    def check_availability(self):
        """30110-CHECK-AVAILABILITY."""
        self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'N' # 30111
        self.WS_BOX_IDX = 1 # Implicit MOVE before PERFORM VARYING
        while self.WS_BOX_IDX <= self.WS_TOTAL_BOXES: # 30112-30118
            if self.BOX_STATUS.get(self.WS_BOX_IDX) == 'A': # 30114
                if self.BOX_SIZE.get(self.WS_BOX_IDX) == self.WS_REQUESTED_SIZE: # 30115
                    self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'Y' # 30116
                    self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX = self.WS_BOX_IDX # 30117
                    break # 30118 EXIT PERFORM
            self.WS_BOX_IDX += 1  # Implicit increment of WS_BOX_IDX
            # The UNTIL condition includes an implied increment
    def assign_box(self):
        """30120-ASSIGN-BOX."""
        self.BOX_STATUS[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX] = 'R' # 30121
        self.BOX_RENTER[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX] = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID # 30122
        self.BOX_RENTAL_DATE[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX] = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE # 30123

    def create_rental_agreement(self):
        """30130-CREATE-RENTAL-AGREEMENT."""
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT = WsRentalAgreement() # 30131
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_box_number = str(self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX) # 30132
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_customer = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID # 30133
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_start_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE # 30134
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_annual_fee = self.WS_BOX_SIZE_FEE.get(self.WS_REQUESTED_SIZE, Decimal("0.00")) # 30135
        self.write_rental_record() # 30136

    def box_access(self):
        """30200-BOX-ACCESS."""
        if self.WS_ACCESS_REQUEST == 'Y': # 30201
            self.verify_renter() # 30202
            if self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED == 'Y': # 30203
                self.log_access() # 30204
                self.escort_to_vault() # 30205

    def verify_renter(self):
        """30210-VERIFY-RENTER."""
        self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'N' # 30211
        if self.BOX_RENTER.get(self.WS_BOX_NUMBER) == self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID: # 30212
            if self.WS_ID_VERIFIED == 'Y': # 30213
                if self.WS_KEY_VERIFIED == 'Y': # 30214
                    self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'Y' # 30215

    def log_access(self):
        """30220-LOG-ACCESS."""
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG = WsAccessLog() # 30221
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_box_number = self.WS_BOX_NUMBER # 30222
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_customer = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID # 30223
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE # 30224
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_time = self.current_time() # 30225
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_type = 'ENTRY' # 30226
        self.write_access_log_record() # 30227

    def escort_to_vault(self):
        """30230-ESCORT-TO-VAULT."""
        self.WS_DISPLAY_MSG = 'VAULT ACCESS GRANTED' # 30231
        self.display_message() # 30232  This assumes display_message handles the display

    def box_drilling(self):
        """30300-BOX-DRILLING."""
        if self.WS_DRILLING_REQUEST == 'Y': # 30301
            self.validate_drilling_auth() # 30302
            if self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED == 'Y': # 30303
                self.schedule_drilling() # 30304
                self.notify_renter() # 30305

    def validate_drilling_auth(self):
        """30310-VALIDATE-DRILLING-AUTH."""
        self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'N' # 30311
        if self.WS_RENT_DELINQUENT_MONTHS >= 12: # 30312
            self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y' # 30313
        if self.WS_COURT_ORDER == 'Y': # 30314
            self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y' # 30315
        if self.WS_DECEASED_RENTER == 'Y': # 30316
            if self.WS_EXECUTOR_VERIFIED == 'Y': # 30317
                self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y' # 30318

    def schedule_drilling(self):
        """30320-SCHEDULE-DRILLING."""
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD = WsDrillingRecord() # 30321
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD.drill_box_number = self.WS_BOX_NUMBER # 30322
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD.drill_reason = self.WS_DRILLING_REASON # 30323
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD.drill_scheduled_date = self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 30 # 30324
        self.write_drilling_record() # 30325

    def notify_renter(self):
        """30330-NOTIFY-RENTER."""
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'BOX-DRILLING' # 30331
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'MAIL' # 30332
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Important notice regarding your safe deposit box' # 30333
        self.send_notification() # 30334

    def box_billing(self):
        """30400-BOX-BILLING."""
        self.WS_BOX_IDX = 1 # Implicit MOVE before PERFORM VARYING
        while self.WS_BOX_IDX <= self.WS_TOTAL_BOXES: # 30401-30407
            if self.BOX_STATUS.get(self.WS_BOX_IDX) == 'R': # 30403
                if self.BOX_RENEWAL_DUE.get(self.WS_BOX_IDX) == 'Y': # 30404
                    self.charge_annual_fee() # 30405
            self.WS_BOX_IDX += 1

    def charge_annual_fee(self):
        """30410-CHARGE-ANNUAL-FEE."""
        self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID = self.BOX_RENTER.get(self.WS_BOX_IDX) # 30411
        self.WS_FEE_AMOUNT = self.BOX_ANNUAL_FEE.get(self.WS_BOX_IDX, Decimal("0.00")) # 30412
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_FEE_AMOUNT # 30413
        self.update_account() # 30414
        self.BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[self.WS_BOX_IDX] = self.BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL.get(self.WS_BOX_IDX, 0) + 10000 # 30415

    def merchant_services(self):
        """31000-MERCHANT-SERVICES."""
        self.process_authorization() # 31001
        self.capture_transaction() # 31002
        self.process_settlement() # 31003
        self.handle_chargeback() # 31004

    def process_authorization(self):
        """31100-PROCESS-AUTHORIZATION."""
        self.validate_card() # 31101
        if self.WS_CARD_VALID == 'Y': # 31102
            self.check_fraud_score() # 31103
            if self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED == 'Y': # 31104
                self.check_available_credit() # 31105
                if self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE == 'Y': # 31106
                    self.approve_auth() # 31107
                else:
                    self.decline_auth() # 31109
            else:
                self.decline_auth() # 31111
        else:
            self.decline_auth() # 31113

    def validate_card(self):
        """31110-VALIDATE-CARD."""
        self.WS_CARD_VALID = 'N' # 31111
        self.check_luhn() # 31112
        if self.WS_LUHN_VALID == 'Y': # 31113
            self.check_expiry() # 31114
            if self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED == 'Y': # 31115
                self.check_cvv() # 31116
                if self.WS_CVV_VALID == 'Y': # 31117
                    self.WS_CARD_VALID = 'Y' # 31118

    def check_luhn(self):
        """31115-CHECK-LUHN."""
        self.WS_LUHN_SUM = 0 # 31116
        self.WS_LUHN_IDX = 16 # Implicit MOVE before PERFORM VARYING
        while self.WS_LUHN_IDX >= 1: # 31117-31128
            self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT = int(self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER[self.WS_LUHN_IDX - 1]) # 31119
            if (17 - self.WS_LUHN_IDX) % 2 == 0: # 31120
                self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT *= 2 # 31121
                if self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT > 9: # 31122
                    self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT -= 9 # 31123
            self.WS_LUHN_SUM += self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT # 31125
            self.WS_LUHN_IDX -= 1  # Implicit decrement of WS_LUHN_IDX
        if self.WS_LUHN_SUM % 10 == 0: # 31129
            self.WS_LUHN_VALID = 'Y' # 31130
        else:
            self.WS_LUHN_VALID = 'N' # 31132

    def check_expiry(self):
        """31116-CHECK-EXPIRY."""
        if self.WS_AUTH_EXPIRY_DATE >= self.WS_PROCESS_DATE: # 31136
            self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'Y' # 31137
        else:
            self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'N' # 31139

    def check_cvv(self):
        """31117-CHECK-CVV."""
        self.cvvverify(self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, self.WS_AUTH_CVV) # CALL 'CVVVERIFY'
        if self.WS_CVV_RESULT == 'M': # 31144
            self.WS_CVV_VALID = 'Y' # 31145
        else:
            self.WS_CVV_VALID = 'N' # 31147

    def check_fraud_score(self):
        """31120-CHECK-FRAUD-SCORE."""
        self.fraudcheck(self.WS_AUTH_REQUEST) # CALL 'FRAUDCHECK'
        if self.FRAUD_SCORE < 70: # 31152
            self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'Y' # 31153
        else:
            self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'N' # 31155
            self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = self.FRAUD_DECLINE_CODE # 31156

    def check_available_credit(self):
        """31130-CHECK-AVAILABLE-CREDIT."""
        self.WS_SEARCH_KEY = self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER # 31159
        self.read_card_account_file() # READ CARD-ACCOUNT-FILE
        if self.WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT >= self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT: # 31161
            self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'Y' # 31162
        else:
            self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'N'

def logging_utilities():
    """99800-LOGGING-UTILITIES."""
    log_info()  # PERFORM 99810-LOG-INFO
    log_warning()  # PERFORM 99820-LOG-WARNING
    log_error()  # PERFORM 99830-LOG-ERROR

def log_info():
    """99810-LOG-INFO."""
    global LOG_LEVEL, WS_LOG_MESSAGE, LOG_TIMESTAMP, LOG_RECORD, WS_LOG_ENTRY
    LOG_LEVEL = 'INFO'  # MOVE 'INFO' TO LOG-LEVEL
    LOG_MESSAGE = WS_LOG_MESSAGE  # MOVE WS-LOG-MESSAGE TO LOG-MESSAGE
    LOG_TIMESTAMP = datetime.now().isoformat()  # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO LOG-TIMESTAMP
    LOG_RECORD = WS_LOG_ENTRY # WRITE LOG-RECORD FROM WS-LOG-ENTRY.
    print(f"INFO: {LOG_MESSAGE} at {LOG_TIMESTAMP}")


def log_warning():
    """99820-LOG-WARNING."""
    global LOG_LEVEL, WS_LOG_MESSAGE, LOG_TIMESTAMP, LOG_RECORD, WS_LOG_ENTRY
    LOG_LEVEL = 'WARN'  # MOVE 'WARN' TO LOG-LEVEL
    LOG_MESSAGE = WS_LOG_MESSAGE  # MOVE WS-LOG-MESSAGE TO LOG-MESSAGE
    LOG_TIMESTAMP = datetime.now().isoformat()  # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO LOG-TIMESTAMP
    LOG_RECORD = WS_LOG_ENTRY # WRITE LOG-RECORD FROM WS-LOG-ENTRY.
    print(f"WARN: {LOG_MESSAGE} at {LOG_TIMESTAMP}")


def log_error():
    """99830-LOG-ERROR."""
    global LOG_LEVEL, WS_LOG_MESSAGE, LOG_TIMESTAMP, LOG_RECORD, WS_LOG_ENTRY
    LOG_LEVEL = 'ERROR'  # MOVE 'ERROR' TO LOG-LEVEL
    LOG_MESSAGE = WS_LOG_MESSAGE  # MOVE WS-LOG-MESSAGE TO LOG-MESSAGE
    LOG_TIMESTAMP = datetime.now().isoformat()  # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO LOG-TIMESTAMP
    LOG_RECORD = WS_LOG_ENTRY # WRITE LOG-RECORD FROM WS-LOG-ENTRY.
    print(f"ERROR: {LOG_MESSAGE} at {LOG_TIMESTAMP}")


def error_handling():
    """99900-ERROR-HANDLING."""
    format_error()  # PERFORM 99910-FORMAT-ERROR
    display_error()  # PERFORM 99920-DISPLAY-ERROR
    write_error_log()  # PERFORM 99930-WRITE-ERROR-LOG

def format_error():
    """99910-FORMAT-ERROR."""
    global WS_FORMATTED_ERROR, WS_ERROR_CODE, WS_ERROR_MSG
    WS_FORMATTED_ERROR = f"ERROR: {WS_ERROR_CODE} - {WS_ERROR_MSG}"  # STRING 'ERROR: ' DELIMITED SIZE ...

        self.ws_projected_income: list[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * 10
        self.ws_projected_losses: list[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * 10
        self.ws_projected_dividends: list[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * 10
        self.ws_projected_capital: list[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * 10

        self.transaction_record: TransactionRecord = TransactionRecord()
        self.ctr_record: CtrRecord = CtrRecord()

        self.sar_pending_record: SarPendingRecord = SarPendingRecord()

        self.bank_statement_item: BankStatementItem = BankStatementItem()
        self.book_transaction: BookTransaction = BookTransaction()
        self.exception_record: ExceptionRecord = ExceptionRecord()
        self.recon_report: ReconReport = ReconReport()

        self.ws_stmt_item_count: int = 0

    def update_cfp_document(self, cfp_overall_status: str, cfp_total_sources: Decimal, cfp_stress_needs: Decimal):
        """
        33330-UPDATE-CFP-DOCUMENT.
        MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-CFP-UPDATE-DATE
        MOVE WS-CFP-STATUS TO CFP-OVERALL-STATUS
        MOVE WS-AVAILABLE-FUNDING TO CFP-TOTAL-SOURCES
        MOVE WS-STRESSED-OUTFLOWS TO CFP-STRESS-NEEDS
        REWRITE CFP-RECORD FROM WS-CFP-DOCUMENT.
        """
        self.ws_cfp_update_date = date.today()
        self.cfp_record.cfp_overall_status = cfp_overall_status
        self.cfp_record.cfp_total_sources = cfp_total_sources
        self.cfp_record.cfp_stress_needs = cfp_stress_needs

        # For demonstration, we'll just print it.
        print(f"CFP Record Updated: {self.cfp_record}")

    def capital_management(self):
        """
        34000-CAPITAL-MANAGEMENT.
        PERFORM 34100-CALCULATE-CAPITAL-RATIOS
        PERFORM 34200-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
        PERFORM 34300-CAPITAL-PLANNING
        PERFORM 34400-STRESS-TESTING.
        """
        self.calculate_capital_ratios()
        self.risk_weighted_assets()
        self.capital_planning()
        self.stress_testing()

    def calculate_capital_ratios(self):
        """
        34100-CALCULATE-CAPITAL-RATIOS.
        PERFORM 34110-CALCULATE-TIER1
        PERFORM 34120-CALCULATE-TIER2
        PERFORM 34130-CALCULATE-RATIOS.
        """
        self.calculate_tier1()
        self.calculate_tier2()
        self.calculate_ratios()

    def calculate_tier1(self):
        """
        34110-CALCULATE-TIER1.
        MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
        ADD WS-COMMON-STOCK TO WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
        ADD WS-RETAINED-EARNINGS TO WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
        ADD WS-AOCI TO WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
        SUBTRACT WS-GOODWILL FROM WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
        SUBTRACT WS-INTANGIBLES FROM WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
        SUBTRACT WS-DTA-DEDUCTION FROM WS-TIER1-CAPITAL.
        """
        self.ws_tier1_capital = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_tier1_capital += self.ws_common_stock
        self.ws_tier1_capital += self.ws_retained_earnings
        self.ws_tier1_capital += self.ws_aoci
        self.ws_tier1_capital -= self.ws_goodwill
        self.ws_tier1_capital -= self.ws_intangibles
        self.ws_tier1_capital -= self.ws_dta_deduction

    def calculate_tier2(self):
        """
        34120-CALCULATE-TIER2.
        MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TIER2-CAPITAL
        ADD WS-SUB-DEBT TO WS-TIER2-CAPITAL
        ADD WS-ALLL-ELIGIBLE TO WS-TIER2-CAPITAL
        COMPUTE WS-TOTAL-CAPITAL = 
           WS-TIER1-CAPITAL + WS-TIER2-CAPITAL.
        """
        self.ws_tier2_capital = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_tier2_capital += self.ws_sub_debt
        self.ws_tier2_capital += self.ws_alll_eligible
        self.ws_total_capital = self.ws_tier1_capital + self.ws_tier2_capital

    def calculate_ratios(self):
        """
        34130-CALCULATE-RATIOS.
        IF WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS > 0
           COMPUTE WS-CET1-RATIO = 
              (WS-TIER1-CAPITAL / WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS) * 100
           COMPUTE WS-CAPITAL-RATIO = 
              (WS-TOTAL-CAPITAL / WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS) * 100
        END-IF
        IF WS-TOTAL-ASSETS > 0
           COMPUTE WS-LEVERAGE-RATIO = 
              (WS-TIER1-CAPITAL / WS-TOTAL-ASSETS) * 100
        END-IF.
        """
        if self.ws_risk_weighted_assets > 0:
            self.ws_cet1_ratio = (self.ws_tier1_capital / self.ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100
            self.ws_capital_ratio = (self.ws_total_capital / self.ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100

        if self.ws_total_assets > 0:
            self.ws_leverage_ratio = (self.ws_tier1_capital / self.ws_total_assets) * 100

    def risk_weighted_assets(self):
        """
        34200-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS.
        MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
        PERFORM 34210-CREDIT-RWA
        PERFORM 34220-MARKET-RWA
        PERFORM 34230-OPERATIONAL-RWA.
        """
        self.ws_risk_weighted_assets = Decimal("0.00")
        self.credit_rwa()
        self.market_rwa()
        self.operational_rwa()

    def credit_rwa(self):
        """
        34210-CREDIT-RWA.
        COMPUTE WS-CASH-RWA = WS-CASH-POSITION * 0.00
        COMPUTE WS-GOVT-RWA = WS-GOVT-SECURITIES * 0.00
        COMPUTE WS-BANK-RWA = WS-BANK-DEPOSITS * 0.20
        COMPUTE WS-MORTGAGE-RWA = WS-RESIDENTIAL-MORTGAGES * 0.50
        COMPUTE WS-COMMERCIAL-RWA = WS-COMMERCIAL-LOANS * 1.00
        COMPUTE WS-CONSUMER-RWA = WS-CONSUMER-LOANS * 1.00
        ADD WS-CASH-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
        ADD WS-GOVT-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
        ADD WS-BANK-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
        ADD WS-MORTGAGE-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
        ADD WS-COMMERCIAL-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
        ADD WS-CONSUMER-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS.
        """
        self.ws_cash_rwa = self.ws_cash_position * Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_govt_rwa = self.ws_govt_securities * Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_bank_rwa = self.ws_bank_deposits * Decimal("0.20")
        self.ws_mortgage_rwa = self.ws_residential_mortgages * Decimal("0.50")
        self.ws_commercial_rwa = self.ws_commercial_loans * Decimal("1.00")
        self.ws_consumer_rwa = self.ws_consumer_loans * Decimal("1.00")

        self.ws_risk_weighted_assets += self.ws_cash_rwa
        self.ws_risk_weighted_assets += self.ws_govt_rwa
        self.ws_risk_weighted_assets += self.ws_bank_rwa
        self.ws_risk_weighted_assets += self.ws_mortgage_rwa
        self.ws_risk_weighted_assets += self.ws_commercial_rwa
        self.ws_risk_weighted_assets += self.ws_consumer_rwa

    def market_rwa(self):
        """
        34220-MARKET-RWA.
        COMPUTE WS-MARKET-RWA = 
           WS-TRADING-ASSETS * WS-MARKET-RISK-FACTOR
        ADD WS-MARKET-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS.
        """
        self.ws_market_rwa = self.ws_trading_assets * self.ws_market_risk_factor
        self.ws_risk_weighted_assets += self.ws_market_rwa

    def operational_rwa(self):
        """
        34230-OPERATIONAL-RWA.
        COMPUTE WS-OPERATIONAL-RWA = 
           WS-GROSS-INCOME * WS-OPERATIONAL-FACTOR * 12.5
        ADD WS-OPERATIONAL-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS.
        """
        self.ws_operational_rwa = self.ws_gross_income * self.ws_operational_factor * Decimal("12.5")
        self.ws_risk_weighted_assets += self.ws_operational_rwa

    def capital_planning(self):
        """
        34300-CAPITAL-PLANNING.
        PERFORM 34310-PROJECT-CAPITAL-NEEDS
        PERFORM 34320-IDENTIFY-CAPITAL-ACTIONS
        PERFORM 34330-UPDATE-CAPITAL-PLAN.
        """
        self.project_capital_needs()
        self.identify_capital_actions()
        self.update_capital_plan()

    def project_capital_needs(self):
        """
        34310-PROJECT-CAPITAL-NEEDS.
        COMPUTE WS-PROJECTED-RWA = 
           WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS * (1 + WS-GROWTH-RATE)
        COMPUTE WS-REQUIRED-CAPITAL = 
           WS-PROJECTED-RWA * WS-TARGET-RATIO / 100
        COMPUTE WS-CAPITAL-GAP = 
           WS-REQUIRED-CAPITAL - WS-TOTAL-CAPITAL.
        """
        self.ws_projected_rwa = self.ws_risk_weighted_assets * (1 + self.ws_growth_rate)
        self.ws_required_capital = self.ws_projected_rwa * self.ws_target_ratio / 100
        self.ws_capital_gap = self.ws_required_capital - self.ws_total_capital

    def identify_capital_actions(self):
        """
        34320-IDENTIFY-CAPITAL-ACTIONS.
        IF WS-CAPITAL-GAP > 0
           EVALUATE TRUE
              WHEN WS-CAPITAL-GAP <= WS-RETAINED-EARNINGS-PROJ
                 MOVE 'ORGANIC GROWTH' TO WS-CAPITAL-ACTION
              WHEN WS-CAPITAL-GAP <= WS-SUB-DEBT-CAPACITY
                 MOVE 'SUB DEBT ISSUANCE' TO WS-CAPITAL-ACTION
              WHEN OTHER
                 MOVE 'EQUITY RAISE' TO WS-CAPITAL-ACTION
           END-EVALUATE
        ELSE
           MOVE 'NO ACTION NEEDED' TO WS-CAPITAL-ACTION
        END-IF.
        """
        if self.ws_capital_gap > 0:
            if self.ws_capital_gap <= self.ws_retained_earnings_proj:
                self.ws_capital_action = 'ORGANIC GROWTH'
            elif self.ws_capital_gap <= self.ws_sub_debt_capacity:
                self.ws_capital_action = 'SUB DEBT ISSUANCE'
            else:
                self.ws_capital_action = 'EQUITY RAISE'
        else:
            self.ws_capital_action = 'NO ACTION NEEDED'

    def update_capital_plan(self):
        """
        34330-UPDATE-CAPITAL-PLAN.
        MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-PLAN-UPDATE-DATE
        MOVE WS-CAPITAL-ACTION TO PLAN-RECOMMENDED-ACTION
        MOVE WS-CAPITAL-GAP TO PLAN-GAP-AMOUNT
        REWRITE CAPITAL-PLAN-RECORD FROM WS-CAPITAL-PLAN.
        """
        self.ws_plan_update_date = date.today()
        self.capital_plan_record.plan_recommended_action = self.ws_capital_action
        self.capital_plan_record.plan_gap_amount = self.ws_capital_gap
        # In a real system, you would rewrite the CAPITAL-PLAN-RECORD to a file or database here.
        # For demonstration, we'll just print it.
        self.ws_capital_plan.plan_recommended_action = self.capital_plan_record.plan_recommended_action
        self.ws_capital_plan.plan_gap_amount = self.capital_plan_record.plan_gap_amount
        print(f"Capital Plan Updated: {self.ws_capital_plan}")

    def stress_testing(self):
        """
        34400-STRESS-TESTING.
        PERFORM 34410-RUN-BASELINE
        PERFORM 34420-RUN-ADVERSE
        PERFORM 34430-RUN-SEVERELY-ADVERSE
        PERFORM 34440-COMPILE-RESULTS.
        """
        self.run_baseline()
        self.run_adverse()
        self.run_severely_adverse()
        self.compile_results()

    def run_baseline(self):
        """
        34410-RUN-BASELINE.
        MOVE 'BASELINE' TO WS-SCENARIO-NAME
        MOVE 0.00 TO WS-RATE-SHOCK

        self.ws_scenario_name = 'BASELINE'
        self.ws_rate_shock = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_gdp_change = Decimal("2.50")
        self.ws_unemployment_rate = Decimal("4.00")
        self.ws_housing_decline = Decimal("0.00")
        self.calculate_stress_impact()

    def perform_if_recon_diff_not_zero(self):
        """
           WS-GL-CONTROL-BAL - WS-SUBLEDGER-TOTAL
           IF WS-RECON-DIFF NOT = ZEROES
              PERFORM 37235-LOG-RECON-EXCEPTION
           END-IF.
        """
        self.WS_RECON_DIFF = self.WS_GL_CONTROL_BAL - self.WS_SUBLEDGER_TOTAL
        if self.WS_RECON_DIFF != Decimal("0.00"):
            self.log_recon_exception()

    def intercompany_recon(self):
        """
        37300-INTERCOMPANY-RECON.
           PERFORM 37310-LOAD-IC-BALANCES
           PERFORM 37320-MATCH-IC-PAIRS
           PERFORM 37330-REPORT-IC-DIFFERENCES.
        """
        self.load_ic_balances()
        self.match_ic_pairs()
        self.report_ic_differences()

    def load_ic_balances(self):
        """
        37310-LOAD-IC-BALANCES.
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-IC-COUNT
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
              READ INTERCOMPANY-FILE INTO WS-IC-BALANCE
                 AT END
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
                 NOT AT END
                    ADD 1 TO WS-IC-COUNT
                    MOVE WS-IC-BALANCE TO 
                       WS-IC-ARRAY(WS-IC-COUNT)
              END-READ
           END-PERFORM
           MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.
        """
        self.WS_IC_COUNT = 0
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG == 'N':
            try:
                self.WS_IC_BALANCE = self.read_intercompany_file()  # Simulate reading
                self.WS_IC_COUNT += 1
                self.WS_IC_ARRAY[self.WS_IC_COUNT - 1] = self.WS_IC_BALANCE
            except EOFError:
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

    def read_intercompany_file(self) -> WS_IC_BALANCE:
        """Simulates reading from a file. Raises EOFError at the end."""
        # Replace with actual file reading logic
        # This is a placeholder, replace with your data source
        if self.WS_IC_COUNT < 5:  # Simulate having 5 records
            ic_balance = WS_IC_BALANCE(
                IC_FROM_ENTITY=f"From{self.WS_IC_COUNT + 1}",
                IC_TO_ENTITY=f"To{self.WS_IC_COUNT + 1}",
                IC_AMOUNT=Decimal(str((self.WS_IC_COUNT + 1) * 100))
            )
            return ic_balance
        else:
            raise EOFError  # Simulate end of file

    def match_ic_pairs(self):
        """
        37320-MATCH-IC-PAIRS.
           PERFORM VARYING WS-IC-IDX FROM 1 BY 1
              UNTIL WS-IC-IDX > WS-IC-COUNT
              PERFORM 37325-FIND-IC-COUNTERPART
           END-PERFORM.
        """
        for self.WS_IC_IDX in range(1, self.WS_IC_COUNT + 1):
            self.find_ic_counterpart()

    def find_ic_counterpart(self):
        """
        37325-FIND-IC-COUNTERPART.
           MOVE IC-FROM-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX) TO WS-SEARCH-FROM
           MOVE IC-TO-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX) TO WS-SEARCH-TO
           PERFORM VARYING WS-IC-IDX2 FROM 1 BY 1
              UNTIL WS-IC-IDX2 > WS-IC-COUNT
              IF IC-FROM-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX2) = WS-SEARCH-TO
                 IF IC-TO-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX2) = WS-SEARCH-FROM
                    COMPUTE WS-IC-DIFF = 
                       IC-AMOUNT(WS-IC-IDX) + 
                       IC-AMOUNT(WS-IC-IDX2)
                    IF WS-IC-DIFF NOT = ZEROES
                       PERFORM 37326-LOG-IC-DIFF
                    END-IF
                    EXIT PERFORM
                 END-IF
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM.
        """
        self.WS_SEARCH_FROM = self.WS_IC_ARRAY[self.WS_IC_IDX - 1].IC_FROM_ENTITY
        self.WS_SEARCH_TO = self.WS_IC_ARRAY[self.WS_IC_IDX - 1].IC_TO_ENTITY
        for self.WS_IC_IDX2 in range(1, self.WS_IC_COUNT + 1):
            if self.WS_IC_ARRAY[self.WS_IC_IDX2 - 1].IC_FROM_ENTITY == self.WS_SEARCH_TO:
                if self.WS_IC_ARRAY[self.WS_IC_IDX2 - 1].IC_TO_ENTITY == self.WS_SEARCH_FROM:
                    self.WS_IC_DIFF = (
                        self.WS_IC_ARRAY[self.WS_IC_IDX - 1].IC_AMOUNT +
                        self.WS_IC_ARRAY[self.WS_IC_IDX2 - 1].IC_AMOUNT
                    )
                    if self.WS_IC_DIFF != Decimal("0.00"):
                        self.log_ic_diff()
                    break  # EXIT PERFORM

    def log_ic_diff(self):
        """
        37326-LOG-IC-DIFF.
           INITIALIZE WS-IC-DIFF-REC
           MOVE WS-SEARCH-FROM TO ICD-FROM
           MOVE WS-SEARCH-TO TO ICD-TO
           MOVE WS-IC-DIFF TO ICD-AMOUNT
           WRITE IC-DIFF-RECORD FROM WS-IC-DIFF-REC.
        """
        self.WS_IC_DIFF_REC = WS_IC_DIFF_REC()  # Initialize
        self.WS_IC_DIFF_REC.ICD_FROM = self.WS_SEARCH_FROM
        self.WS_IC_DIFF_REC.ICD_TO = self.WS_SEARCH_TO
        self.WS_IC_DIFF_REC.ICD_AMOUNT = self.WS_IC_DIFF
        self.write_ic_diff_record(self.WS_IC_DIFF_REC)

    def write_ic_diff_record(self, record: WS_IC_DIFF_REC):
        """Simulates writing to a file"""
        print(f"Writing to {self.IC_DIFF_RECORD}: {record}")

    def log_user_action(self):
        """
        38100-LOG-USER-ACTION.
           INITIALIZE WS-AUDIT-RECORD
           COMPUTE WS-AUDIT-ID = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999999999
           MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-AUDIT-TIMESTAMP
           MOVE WS-USER-ID TO WS-AUDIT-USER
           MOVE WS-ACTION-TYPE TO WS-AUDIT-ACTION
           MOVE WS-SESSION-ID TO WS-AUDIT-SESSION-ID
           WRITE AUDIT-RECORD FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD.
        """
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD = WS_AUDIT_RECORD()  # Initialize
        self.WS_AUDIT_ID = Decimal(str(random.random() * 99999999999))
        self.WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP = datetime.now().isoformat()
        self.WS_AUDIT_USER = self.WS_USER_ID
        self.WS_AUDIT_ACTION = self.WS_ACTION_TYPE
        self.WS_AUDIT_SESSION_ID = self.WS_SESSION_ID
        self.write_audit_record(self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD)

    def archive_audit_logs(self):
        """
        38400-ARCHIVE-AUDIT-LOGS.
           IF WS-END-OF-MONTH = 'Y'
              PERFORM 38410-MOVE-TO-ARCHIVE
              PERFORM 38420-COMPRESS-ARCHIVE
           END-IF.
        """
        if self.WS_END_OF_MONTH == 'Y':
            self.move_to_archive()
            self.compress_archive()

    def move_to_archive(self):
        """
        38410-MOVE-TO-ARCHIVE.
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
              READ AUDIT-FILE INTO WS-AUDIT-RECORD
                 AT END
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
                 NOT AT END
                    IF WS-AUDIT-TIMESTAMP < WS-ARCHIVE-DATE
                       WRITE ARCHIVE-AUDIT-RECORD 
                          FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD
                       DELETE AUDIT-FILE
                    END-IF
              END-READ
           END-PERFORM
           MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.
        """
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG == 'N':
            try:
                self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD = self.read_audit_file()
                if self.WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP < self.WS_ARCHIVE_DATE:
                    self.write_archive_audit_record(self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD)
                    self.delete_audit_file()
            except EOFError:
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

    def read_audit_file(self) -> WS_AUDIT_RECORD:
        """Simulates reading from a file.  Raises EOFError at the end."""
        # Replace with actual file reading logic
        # This is a placeholder
        if self.WS_NOSTRO_COUNT < 3:
            return WS_AUDIT_RECORD()
        else:
            raise EOFError

         """Simulates writing to a file"""
         print(f"Writing to {self.ARCHIVE_AUDIT_RECORD}: {record}")

    def performance_monitoring(self):
        """
        39000-PERFORMANCE-MONITORING.
           PERFORM 39100-COLLECT-METRICS
           PERFORM 39200-ANALYZE-PERFORMANCE
